<p class='hn-title'>(9) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.derekthompson.org/p/the-death-of-the-american-host' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Everybody’s home. No one’s coming over</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>人人都在家，却没人来访</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49891295' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 804</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49891295' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 718</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Americans are hosting less than before, with more people staying home and fewer dinner invitations circulating. The piece links this to longer work hours, streaming culture, and social fragmentation, and considers what hospitality, community, and local economies lose when in-person gatherings fade.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>美国的接待文化正在衰退，越来越多人呆在家里，但请客来访的次数在减少。文章将工作压力、数字娱乐和社交碎片化与线下聚会的下降联系起来，讨论这对社区生活、依赖聚会的小型企业以及何谓“好客”的影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!Hczb!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Fefc21223-6d44-45bb-84be-7a80c50acc8a_2014x1332.png' data-full='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!Hczb!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Fefc21223-6d44-45bb-84be-7a80c50acc8a_2014x1332.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49949438' data-tags='Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(10) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49949438' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Tell HN: Bob Cringely has died</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>鲍勃·克林西利去世（Bob Cringely）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49949438' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 799</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49949438' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 173</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Bob Cringely, a veteran tech journalist and columnist, has died. He helped popularize accessible, narrative-driven tech journalism with works like I, Cringely and commentary on Apple and Silicon Valley, shaping how mainstream audiences think about the industry. His passing marks the end of an era for journalism that mixes insider storytelling with tech analysis.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>资深科技记者鲍勃·克林西利去世。他以 I, Cringely 等作品和对苹果及科技行业的评论，推动了以叙事方式讲述科技的风格，影响了主流对科技产业的理解。他的离世标志着一种将行业分析与个人叙事相结合的新闻传统的告别。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49927754' data-tags='Legal,Privacy,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(11) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.eff.org/deeplinks/2026/10/court-agrees-eff-utahs-vpn-law-demands-technical-impossibility' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Court agrees with EFF: Utah&#x27;s VPN law demands a technical impossibility</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>法院认定犹他州的 VPN 法等同技术不可实现</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49927754' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 783</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49927754' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 388</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The court sided with the EFF, ruling that Utah&#x27;s VPN law imposes requirements that are effectively impossible to meet. The decision underscores how prescriptive tech laws can clash with real-world capabilities of privacy tools. For engineers and policymakers, it&#x27;s a reminder that regulatory designs must align with practical feasibility.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>法院支持EFF的立场，裁定犹他州的 VPN 法在技术实现上不可行。此判决凸显了规制性科技法规与隐私工具现实能力之间的冲突。对工程师和政策制定者而言，这是一个提醒：可执行的规则须与实际可行性相符。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.eff.org/files/banner_library/vpn-highway-tunnel-banner.jpg' data-full='https://www.eff.org/files/banner_library/vpn-highway-tunnel-banner.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(12) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://america.gov/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>America.gov</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>America.gov 政府信息门户</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49893509' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 737</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49893509' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 670</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>America.gov consolidates official U.S. government information into a central portal to improve public access to policies, data, and services. The platform aims to streamline citizen engagement and provide clear, official guidance across agencies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>America.gov 将美国政府的官方信息集中到一个门户，提升公众获取政策、数据与服务的便利性。该平台旨在简化公民互动，提供跨机构的清晰官方指引。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49896604' data-tags='AI,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(13) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://openai.com/index/introducing-dots/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Dots: Always-on agents</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Dots：持续运行的智能代理</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49896604' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 724</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49896604' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 613</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI unveils Dots, a framework for persistent, always-on agents that can operate continuously with long term memory and coordinated actions. This could enable more autonomous AI workflows and real-time decision making, but raises questions about safety, resource use, and privacy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI 推出 Dots，支持持续运行、具备长期记忆的智能代理，能够持续执行任务并协调行动。这一能力可能推动更高效的自动化工作流和实时决策，但也引发安全、资源消耗和隐私方面的讨论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://images.ctfassets.net/kftzwdyauwt9/3SQQ9jqSm3aytzUPKuNS3X/e30d9ff756987b178e2b141de5c64a35/Art_Card___Landscape___16_9___1920_1080.png?w=1600&amp;h=900&amp;fit=fill' data-full='https://images.ctfassets.net/kftzwdyauwt9/3SQQ9jqSm3aytzUPKuNS3X/e30d9ff756987b178e2b141de5c64a35/Art_Card___Landscape___16_9___1920_1080.png?w=1600&amp;h=900&amp;fit=fill' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49880036' data-tags='Entertainment,Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(14) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mubi.com/en/notebook/posts/pirating-the-pirates' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Pirating the Pirates</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>盗版海盗</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49880036' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 680</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49880036' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 350</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Entertainment'>Entertainment</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A notebook piece exploring piracy culture and its impact on film distribution, examining how piracy ecosystems form, their economics for independent cinema, and what content creators can learn about audience behavior and distribution strategies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>通过对盗版文化与电影发行的探讨，揭示盗版生态如何形成、对独立电影生存的影响，以及内容创作者在观众行为与发行策略方面可以学到的经验。</p>

</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49942706' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 642</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49942706' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 322</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Kolibri is presented as a sovereign open-weight model, designed for on-premise deployment with privacy and governance controls.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Kolibri 被定位为一款主权开放权重的模型，允许企业在本地部署以实现隐私与治理控制。文章介绍了许可、安全措施以及在私有环境中的部署选项。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(17) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nytimes.com/athletic/7648198/2026/10/01/mike-tomlin-minecraft-nfl-coach/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Mike Tomlin spent 12 years building a Minecraft city</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>迈克·汤姆林花12 年打造 Minecraft 城市</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49925184' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 633</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49925184' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 154</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>New York Times profiles NFL coach Mike Tomlin’s 12-year Minecraft city project, revealing how long-form world-building parallels leadership, teamwork, and problem-solving in coaching. The piece offers a surprising window into how hobbies can inform professional skills.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>纽约时报报道 NFL 教练迈克·汤姆林花费 12 年构建 Minecraft 城市的事迹，揭示长期世界建设与领导力、团队协作以及解决问题能力之间的共鸣。文章为展示爱好也能为职业技能提供启示。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://static01.nyt.com/athletic/uploads/wp/2026/10/01110103/1001_Tomlin1.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;fit=crop' data-full='https://static01.nyt.com/athletic/uploads/wp/2026/10/01110103/1001_Tomlin1.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;fit=crop' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(18) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.cloudflare.com/clef-decision-models/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Clef: Open-weight decision models, and new RL fine-tuning platform</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Clef：开源决策模型与新的 RL 微调平台</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49923692' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 622</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49923692' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 215</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Clef introduces open-source decision models and a new reinforcement learning fine-tuning platform, aiming to make RL workflows more accessible and reproducible. The combination could accelerate research and development of controllable AI systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Clef 推出开源决策模型，以及用于强化学习微调的新平台，旨在让 RL 工作流更易用、可重复性更强。这一组合有望推动可控 AI 系统的研究与开发。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blog.cloudflare.com/_emdash/api/media/file/01M3TJV43SPQCPKJ6GBXFCDKNE.01M3TJV53VYDMVNCZDPH1FBFYN.png' data-full='https://blog.cloudflare.com/_emdash/api/media/file/01M3TJV43SPQCPKJ6GBXFCDKNE.01M3TJV53VYDMVNCZDPH1FBFYN.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(19) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://calnewport.com/its-time-to-investigate-the-ai-labs/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>It&#x27;s Time to Investigate the AI Labs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>是时候调查 AI 实验室了</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A call to audit AI labs’ practices, funding, and safety standards, advocating transparency and independent oversight of AI research and development.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>呼吁对 AI 实验室的实践、资金来源与安全标准进行审计，倡导透明度与独立监督以确保研究安全与合规。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49920160' data-tags='Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(20) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/streetcomplete/StreetComplete/issues/5421' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>StreetComplete on iOS is now in public beta</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>StreetComplete iOS 公测现已开启</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49920160' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 611</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49920160' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 168</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>StreetComplete has launched a public beta on iOS, expanding its task-based workflow that lets users contribute OpenStreetMap data by answering simple questions about streets and landmarks. The move broadens access beyond Android and could accelerate map data improvements, though testers should expect typical beta churn as the app matures.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>StreetComplete 已在 iOS 发布公开测试，扩大了通过回答简单问题来贡献 OpenStreetMap 数据的任务驱动工作流。此次扩平台可望推动社区贡献并让 Android 之外的用户加入，但测试阶段仍会有常见的不稳定和本地化需要改进的问题。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/1214691b40da9a4bc4a5f40865a62f189fb798e48ff011b7e4a9cad9eaca61af/streetcomplete/StreetComplete/issues/5421' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/1214691b40da9a4bc4a5f40865a62f189fb798e48ff011b7e4a9cad9eaca61af/streetcomplete/StreetComplete/issues/5421' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49868830' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 584</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49868830' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 248</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Ember-1 appears to be a project in the Ember ecosystem.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这是 Ember 生态系统中名为 Ember-1 的一个项目的简要介绍。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://fireworks.ai/images/OpenGraph.png' data-full='https://fireworks.ai/images/OpenGraph.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49949235' data-tags='Business,Finance,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(22) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://simonwillison.net/2026/Oct/3/default-hard-budget-caps/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>We&#x27;re going to need default hard budget caps on pretty much everything</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我们需要对几乎所有事情设定默认的硬性预算上限</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49949235' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 576</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49949235' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 294</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Advocating a policy of hard budget caps to prevent runaway costs in tech projects and experiments. The piece argues for automatic limits on compute, storage, and spending, plus tooling to enforce caps without stifling creativity. It positions budget discipline as essential for sustainable experimentation in AI and cloud-native stacks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者主张对几乎所有技术支出设定默认的硬性预算上限，以防止成本失控。文章提出在计算、存储等方面设定自动约束，并提供执行工具，既控制支出又不完全扼杀创新，强调预算约束在可持续创新中的作用。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49953495' data-tags='AI,Privacy,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(23) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/Niko1221/Strata' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Run Qwen 3.8 Flash Next (125B) on consumer hardware (RTX 4090) at 100T/s</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在消费级显卡 RTX 4090 上实现 Qwen 3.8 Flash Next 125B 模型的 100T/s 吞吐</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49953495' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 573</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49953495' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 274</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An experimental setup shows Qwen 3.8 Flash Next 125B running on a single RTX 4090 with extremely high throughput, around 100T/s. The work leverages optimized kernels and model quantization, illustrating how multi-hundred-billion-parameter models may become feasible on consumer hardware for research and prototyping. This pushes the boundaries of on-device AI, enabling privacy-preserving demos and edge deployments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>通过实验设置显示，125B 版本的 Qwen 3.8 Flash Next 能在单块 RTX 4090 上实现极高吞吐，约 100T/s。该工作利用优化内核与模型量化，展示了在消费级硬件上运行大模型的可行性，为研究、原型开发以及隐私保护的边缘部署提供了可能。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/027794c268e81797b30854290ac35d34de80b7e369031c1444a21cfc7607dcee/Niko1221/Strata' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/027794c268e81797b30854290ac35d34de80b7e369031c1444a21cfc7607dcee/Niko1221/Strata' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49892245' data-tags='Energy,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(24) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://spectrum.ieee.org/delhi-electricity-loss' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How Delhi cut electricity loss from 50 to 5 percent</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>德里如何将电力损耗从50%降至5%</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49892245' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 571</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49892245' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 309</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Delhi dramatically reduced electricity distribution losses from about 50% to around 5% through targeted reforms, grid upgrades, and better metering. The move showcases how smart infrastructure and governance can unlock huge efficiency gains for urban utilities and could serve as a scalable model for other cities.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>德里通过有针对性的改革、输配电网升级和改进计量，将电力损耗从约50%降至约5%左右。此举展示了智慧基础设施与治理在提升城市电力效率方面的潜力，并具有可复制性供其他城市参考。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://spectrum.ieee.org/media-library/image.jpg?id=67793961&amp;width=1200&amp;height=600&amp;coordinates=0%2C250%2C0%2C250' data-full='https://spectrum.ieee.org/media-library/image.jpg?id=67793961&amp;width=1200&amp;height=600&amp;coordinates=0%2C250%2C0%2C250' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49883844' data-tags='AI,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(25) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/firelex/jeff' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Jeff – Jev-compatible 0.8B decision models, trained at home, ~30 ms</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Jeff：Jev 兼容的0.8B决策模型，在家训练，推理约30毫秒</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49883844' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 569</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49883844' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 222</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Jeff introduces Jev-compatible 0.8B decision models that can be trained at home and run inference in about 30 ms. The lightweight scale enables on-device experimentation, lowering barriers for individuals and small teams to prototype decision systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Jeff 提供 Jev 兼容的 0.8B 决策模型，可在本地训练，推理时延约 30 毫秒。此举降低了个人和小团队在本地进行 AI 原型开发的门槛，凸显对隐私和本地化计算友好的趋势。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/58007a3c610cd76359a2607408649873459234efe21b764d80c3b27618864eb2/firelex/jeff' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/58007a3c610cd76359a2607408649873459234efe21b764d80c3b27618864eb2/firelex/jeff' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49928121' data-tags='Security,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(26) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lwn.net/Articles/1097401/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Several vulnerabilities have been discovered in the Linux kernel</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Linux 内核曝出多项漏洞</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49928121' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 563</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49928121' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 404</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers have disclosed several vulnerabilities affecting the Linux kernel, potentially enabling privilege escalation or data leakage. Patch availability and response timing will vary by distribution, so admins should test updates promptly. The disclosure underscores the ongoing drive to harden core OS components against emerging threat vectors.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究人员披露多项影响 Linux 内核的漏洞，可能造成权限提升或数据泄露。各发行版的修复时间不同，系统管理员应尽快测试并应用更新。此次披露凸显持续对核心操作系统组件进行加固的必要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://static.lwn.net/images/logo/barepenguin-70.webp' data-full='https://static.lwn.net/images/logo/barepenguin-70.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(27) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://developer.apple.com/pass-designer/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Apple Pass Designer</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Apple Pass 设计器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49937276' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 558</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49937276' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 333</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Apple Pass Designer provides a visual editor for Wallet passes, letting developers customize fields, images, and templates with a real-time preview. It streamlines creating compliant passes for iPhone wallets, reducing boilerplate code.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Apple Pass Designer 提供用于 Wallet 通行证的可视化编辑器，开发者可自定义字段、图像和模板并获得实时预览。它简化了符合规范的通行证创建，减少了样板代码。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://developer.apple.com/news/images/og/pass-designer-og.png' data-full='https://developer.apple.com/news/images/og/pass-designer-og.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(28) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.frogandtoad.ai/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Frog and Toad and the Increasingly Capable Machines</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>青蛙和蟾蜍与日益强大的机器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49927760' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 558</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49927760' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 128</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A playful take on how increasingly capable machines are reshaping work and creativity, the piece argues that AI&#x27;s expanding capabilities are changing what is possible for individuals and teams. It considers implications for collaboration, productivity, and the boundaries between human and machine intelligence.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇文章以轻松笔触讨论日益强大的机器正在如何重塑工作与创造力。文章探讨了对协作、生产力以及人机智能边界的影响。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49896050' data-tags='AI,Privacy,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(29) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.eff.org/deeplinks/2026/09/draftkings-using-ai-supercharge-harms-online-behavioral-advertising' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>DraftKings is using AI to behaviorally target chronic gamblers</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>DraftKings 正在用 AI 针对慢性赌徒进行行为定向</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49896050' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 554</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49896050' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 420</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>DraftKings is using AI to tailor advertising based on chronic gamblers’ behavior, aiming to maximize engagement and spending. This raises urgent ethical and regulatory concerns about predatory targeting, calling for stronger safeguards, transparency, and oversight to protect vulnerable users.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>DraftKings 正在利用 AI 根据慢性赌徒的行为特征对广告进行定向，以提升参与度和支出。这一做法引发关于掠夺性定向、伦理与监管的紧迫关切，需加强保护措施、透明度和监管以保护脆弱用户。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.eff.org/files/banner_library/mobile-privacy.png' data-full='https://www.eff.org/files/banner_library/mobile-privacy.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(30) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.gitbutler.com/git-3-sha-256' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Git 3.0&#x27;s upcoming SHA-256 default will be a costly mistake</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Git 3.0 的默认 SHA-256 将是代价高昂的错误</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49924179' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 552</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49924179' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 530</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Git 3.0 moving to SHA-256 default is criticized as introducing costs in perf, storage, and compatibility; the piece argues for considering migration impact and tooling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章批评 Git 3.0 将默认哈希改为 SHA-256 的决定，可能带来性能、存储与向后兼容性的成本，呼吁权衡迁移影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://gitbutler-docs-images-public.s3.us-east-1.amazonaws.com/git-3-sha-256.webp' data-full='https://gitbutler-docs-images-public.s3.us-east-1.amazonaws.com/git-3-sha-256.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49877988' data-tags='Programming,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(31) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.alexewerlof.com/p/coding-is-not-solved' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Coding is not solved</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>编码问题尚未解决</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49877988' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 534</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49877988' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 521</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Code remains a hard problem despite tooling progress; writing correct, maintainable software, with scalable architecture and solid processes, is still challenging. AI can help but cannot replace the craft of software engineering or the need for disciplined practices.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>尽管工具与AI辅助不断进步，编码仍然是一个难题；正确、可维护且具可扩展性的系统设计与流程依然具有挑战性。AI 能帮助，但无法取代软件工程的工艺性与规范性实践。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!-Pk4!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F409bf75a-d9ef-4adf-b242-bee9d2664a24_1280x1247.jpeg' data-full='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!-Pk4!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F409bf75a-d9ef-4adf-b242-bee9d2664a24_1280x1247.jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49881747' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 530</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49881747' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 177</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Entertainment'>Entertainment</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A humorous take on the idea of Windows 11.5, lampooning release cadence, feature promises, and the never-ending iterations of Windows. It riffs on user frustration and the gap between hype and usability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>以对 Windows 11.5 的讽刺笔记为载体，调侃版本迭代节奏、功能承诺与实际使用之间的错位，反映用户对系统变更的情绪与现实体验的落差。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(33) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://tomwojcik.com/posts/2026-09-21/september-2026-the-world-today/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>September 2026: The world today, as seen by one Polish guy</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>2026年9月：一个波兰人眼中的世界</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49905487' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 502</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49905487' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 379</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>From a Polish vantage point, the post surveys how September 2026 reshapes tech policy, AI adoption, and global power dynamics. It blends on-the-ground observations with macro signals about EU regulation and startup funding. The takeaway is a cautious view of where innovation and geopolitics intersect.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>从波兰视角出发，文章梳理了2026年9月全球科技政策、AI采用与地缘政治格局的变化。结合实地观察与宏观信号，分析欧盟监管与初创资金的趋势。核心在于警觉地看待创新与地缘政治如何交汇。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49891480' data-tags='Privacy,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(34) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theguardian.com/technology/2026/sep/29/trial-live-facial-recognition-cameras-london-stations-false-positive' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>500k facial scans at UK stations yield no arrests, 1 false positive</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>英国车站的五十万次人脸识别未逮捕，仅一例误报</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49891480' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 501</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49891480' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 299</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A large-scale facial recognition test at UK rail stations produced no arrests and just one false positive from half a million scans. The result highlights limited predictive value for crime prevention and rekindles debates over privacy and surveillance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>英国铁路站点的大规模人脸识别测试在累计约50万次扫描中未逮捕任何人，仅有一次误报。结果凸显对治安预防的实际效用有限，再次引发对隐私与监控的讨论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/59a72cd2826be7e1e72f407ba3a515ec43697227/546_44_4210_3370/master/4210.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=0c92b09e04de7a9aa71a1822d47b9589' data-full='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/59a72cd2826be7e1e72f407ba3a515ec43697227/546_44_4210_3370/master/4210.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=0c92b09e04de7a9aa71a1822d47b9589' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-title'>(38) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.thisamericanlife.org/897/transcript' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Kids turned low-traffic NPR Spotify comments into a secret group chat</a></p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This American Life transcripts reveal kids turning sparse NPR Spotify comments into a private chat, illustrating how public spaces can be repurposed for covert coordination. It highlights how youth repurpose platforms to form private communities with real-world implications.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>节目文本揭示孩子们把 NPR 的 Spotify 评论区变成秘密群聊，展示公开空间如何被重新利用以实现隐蔽协作。这也揭示了青少年在平台上创建私密社群及其对现实的影响。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(39) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.phoronix.com/news/XDC-2026-Valve-Timur-AMDGPU' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The work by Valve&#x27;s Timur Kristóf on improving old AMD GPUs on Linux</a></p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Valve engineer Timur Kristóf showcased work to improve older AMD GPUs on Linux, aiming for better performance and stability. The effort exemplifies ongoing collaboration between game platforms and open-source drivers to extend hardware lifecycles and keep Linux gaming viable.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Valve 的 Timur Kristóf 展示了在 Linux 上改进老旧 AMD GPU 的工作，目标是提升性能与稳定性。这体现了游戏平台开发者与开源驱动团队的持续合作，延长硬件寿命并保持 Linux 游戏生态。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49948254' data-tags='Privacy,Legal'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(40) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://techcrunch.com/2026/10/03/federal-judge-calls-flock-indiscriminate-mass-surveillance/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Federal judge calls Flock &#x27;indiscriminate mass surveillance&#x27;</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>联邦法官称 Flock 为无差别群体监控</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49948254' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 455</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49948254' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 249</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A federal judge characterized Flock&#x27;s surveillance technology as indiscriminate mass surveillance, highlighting privacy and civil-liberties concerns. The ruling adds material legal pressure on how automated video capture can be deployed by law enforcement and could influence procurement policies and oversight.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一名联邦法官将 Flock 的监控技术描述为无差别群体监控，突显对隐私与公民自由的担忧。此裁决对执法部门的自动化视频捕捉部署施加法律压力，可能影响采购与监督政策。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://techcrunch.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/flock-camera-pole.jpg?resize=1200,799' data-full='https://techcrunch.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/flock-camera-pole.jpg?resize=1200,799' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49940394' data-tags='Gaming,Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(41) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.newgrounds.com/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Newgrounds.com – A community of games, music, and art</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Newgrounds：一个游戏、音乐与艺术社区</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49940394' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 454</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49940394' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 135</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Newgrounds remains a long-running hub for indie games, music, and art, built on user-generated content and a tight creator community. It helped shape early web animation and Flash-era games and continues to influence modern indie developers and online culture.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Newgrounds 长期以来一直是独立游戏、音乐与艺术的聚集地，由用户生成内容驱动。它在早期网页动画和 Flash 时代游戏中发挥了重要作用，至今仍影响着现代独立开发者和网络文化。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(42) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theatlantic.com/technology/2026/10/openai-safety-team-resignation/688881/?gift=v5U_UzUTothfWXsPxtvNVAh7esWToMRD6XnbXmc5WgA' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I quit OpenAI because its culture is broken</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我离开 OpenAI，因为文化已经崩坏</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49944227' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 439</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49944227' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 740</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An account of internal culture issues at OpenAI, including safety team resignations and disagreements over priorities. The piece underscores how organizational culture can shape safety practices, product strategy, and talent retention in AI companies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>讲述 OpenAI 内部文化问题、安全团队辞职及对公司优先级的分歧。文章强调组织文化对 AI 公司的安全实践、产品路线选择与人才留任的影响。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49906432' data-tags='Politics,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(43) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://twitter.com/tuakdotsol/status/2105105417760391258' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Singapore govt dating app uses Gale-Shapley stable marriage algorithm</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>新加坡政府的约会应用采用稳定婚配算法（Gale-Shapley）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49906432' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 435</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49906432' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 439</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Singapore&#x27;s government dating app uses the Gale-Shapley stable marriage algorithm to optimize matches. The approach raises questions about privacy, consent, and fairness in algorithmic matchmaking while illustrating how formal matching theory can inform consumer apps.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>新加坡政府的交友应用采用稳定婚配算法（Gale-Shapley）来优化配对。该做法引发关于隐私、同意和算法公平性的讨论，同时展示了正式匹配理论在消费级应用中的应用潜力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://pbs.twimg.com/profile_images/1996014367368966144/78LfGHq7_400x400.jpg' data-full='https://pbs.twimg.com/profile_images/1996014367368966144/78LfGHq7_400x400.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49875148' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(44) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://thecivilian.co.nz/2026/09/27/ai-companies-in-fierce-arms-race-to-demonstrate-their-model-is-the-most-existentially-threatening-to-humanity/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI companies in race to demonstrate their model most threatening to humanity</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 公司竞相证明其模型对人类威胁最大</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49875148' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 434</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49875148' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 392</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AI firms are racing to demonstrate which model poses the greatest existential threat to humanity, a framing that influences policy, funding, and public trust. This competition blends safety rhetoric with competitive signaling, sometimes obscuring real risk assessments. The takeaway is that governance and independent evaluation matter as much as capability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>AI 公司正在竞相证明其模型对人类构成最大的生存威胁，这种论调会影响政策、资金与公众信任。此类竞赛把安全口号和竞争信号混合在一起，常常掩盖真实风险评估。关键在于治理与独立评估的重要性，与能力两者同等重要。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i0.wp.com/thecivilian.co.nz/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/aiendoftheworldstory.jpg?fit=872%2C591&amp;ssl=1' data-full='https://i0.wp.com/thecivilian.co.nz/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/aiendoftheworldstory.jpg?fit=872%2C591&amp;ssl=1' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49890226' data-tags='Privacy,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(45) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://jorgegarciaherrero.com/wp-content/interactivos/20260916-Prompt-like-a-butterfly-sting-like-a-tracker-(clean).pdf' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A Privacy Analysis of Web and Mobile Conversational AI Agents [pdf]</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>网络与移动端对话式人工智能代理的隐私分析</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49890226' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 420</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49890226' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 136</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A detailed privacy analysis of web and mobile conversational AI agents reveals how these systems collect data, process transcripts, and may expose users to privacy risks. The report highlights issues like data sharing with third parties, retention policies, and potential prompt leakage, underscoring the need for stricter minimization, transparency, and privacy-preserving design.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对网页及移动端对话式人工智能代理的数据收集、传输与存储等隐私风险进行了深入分析，揭示潜在的敏感信息泄露与第三方数据共享等问题。强调需要更严格的数据最小化、透明度与隐私保护设计，以及改进的访问控制与用户权利。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49943034' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 415</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49943034' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 12</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Kolibri is Aleph Alpha&#x27;s sovereign LLM designed to run on private infrastructure inside Germany, giving organizations control over data and governance. It details its architecture, training choices, and safety mechanisms that support enterprise deployments while meeting regulatory expectations. The story highlights Europe’s push for AI autonomy and vendor independence in AI.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Kolibri 是 Aleph Alpha 的自主大语言模型，设计用于在德国的私有基础设施中运行，以实现数据与治理的控制权。文章讲解了其架构、训练选择和安全机制，支持面向企业的部署并符合监管要求。此事凸显欧洲在 AI 主权与供应商独立性方面的努力。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An exploration of the electrician trade as a model of skilled, hands-on work and problem-solving. The piece contrasts traditional trades with software careers, highlighting discipline, apprenticeship, and safety as transferable skills for technical work. It argues that the craft offers a different path into engineering that rewards practical intuition and precise execution.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文对电工这一技艺进行了深度探讨，强调动手实践、学徒制和安全意识等技能在技术工作中的价值。与软件行业的常规路径相比，电工的精益求精和实操直觉同样是进入工程领域的有力路径，适合喜欢动手的读者。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AI competition has hit an awkward moment where hype clashes with practical limits, safety, and governance. Companies push scale while grappling with data, compute costs, and credible benchmarks, underscoring the need for transparent metrics and responsible deployment.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>AI竞赛进入尴尬阶段，炒作与现实限制造成紧张局面。各方在追求规模的同时，需面对数据、算力成本与可信基准的挑战，强调需要更透明的评估标准与负责任的落地。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(54) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arstechnica.com/tech-policy/2026/09/immigration-advocate-sues-border-agents-for-demanding-his-cell-phone/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Returning from vacation? The government can search your phone without a warrant</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>度假回来后？政府可在没有搜查令的情况下搜索你的手机</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49920234' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 391</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49920234' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 364</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An immigration advocate is suing border agents for demanding his cell phone data after returning from vacation, arguing such searches violate privacy rights. The case spotlights the tension between digital privacy and border enforcement, and could influence future policy on device searches at points of entry.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一名移民倡导者在返回后被边境人员要求交出手机数据提起诉讼，质疑此类搜索是否侵犯隐私。此案凸显数字隐私与边境执法之间的张力，可能影响未来对入境时设备搜查的政策。</p>

</div>

</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(55) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.worksinprogress.news/p/why-really-caused-the-bronze-age' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why the Bronze Age Collapsed</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>青铜时代为何崩溃</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49890732' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 386</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49890732' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 287</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Scholars synthesize climate, trade, and social factors to argue the Bronze Age collapse was multi-causal rather than caused by a single trigger.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>学者综合气候、贸易与社会因素，认为青铜时代崩溃是多因素综合作用的结果，而非单一原因。此文强调古代社会的连锁失败如何为现代韧性规划提供启示。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!Uazu!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F706f94b0-b620-4a80-8d84-e5c0c08fcc1b_2560x1707.webp' data-full='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!Uazu!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2F706f94b0-b620-4a80-8d84-e5c0c08fcc1b_2560x1707.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49883539' data-tags='Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(56) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.kqed.org/news/12101534/california-farmers-are-struggling-to-sell-grapes-as-demand-for-wine-drops' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>California farmers are struggling to sell grapes as demand for wine drops</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>加州农民因葡萄酒需求下降而难以销售葡萄</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49883539' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 383</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49883539' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 965</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Wine demand falls, squeezing California grape growers as prices slip and supply overshoots demand. Some farmers pivot to alternative uses like table grapes, juice, or raisins, illustrating how shifts in consumer tastes ripple through agriculture and supply chains.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>酒类需求下降导致加州葡萄种植者收入受压，葡萄价格走低、供应过剩。部分农民转向鲜食葡萄、果汁或葡萄干等替代用途，展现出消费者口味变化对农业生产和供应链的影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.kqed.org/wp-content/uploads/sites/10/2026/06/20260610_LodiWine_GC-26-KQED.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.kqed.org/wp-content/uploads/sites/10/2026/06/20260610_LodiWine_GC-26-KQED.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49923466' data-tags='Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(57) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://turbopuffer.com/blog/rip-vector-database' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>RIP, vector database</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>向量数据库：再见</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49923466' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 383</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49923466' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 114</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A provocative take arguing that the era of dedicated vector databases may be fading as search and embedding work converge. The piece weighs cost, complexity, and performance trade-offs, suggesting that robust vector capabilities can live inside existing databases or newer hybrid engines.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇文章质疑专用向量数据库的长期价值，认为搜索与嵌入技术正在合并，成败取决于成本、复杂性和性能。作者提出，将向量功能整合到现有数据库或使用混合型引擎，可能比维护独立向量数据库更高效。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://turbopuffer.com/og/turbopuffer.png' data-full='https://turbopuffer.com/og/turbopuffer.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49891550' data-tags='Politics,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(58) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.tomshardware.com/software/the-netherlands-is-rolling-alternative-nixos-based-software-ecosystem-after-u-s-sanctions-on-icc-took-microsoft-off-the-table-trial-programs-running-now-first-release-expected-at-end-of-2027' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>US sanctions force The Netherlands off Microsoft and toward alternative NixOS</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美国制裁迫使荷兰放弃微软，转向 NixOS 为基础的替代方案</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49891550' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 382</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49891550' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 388</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>US sanctions are nudging the Netherlands to move away from Microsoft toward an open-source NixOS-based software ecosystem. The shift could affect government procurement, software sovereignty, and ongoing modernization efforts.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>美国制裁促使荷兰摆脱微软，转向以 NixOS 为基础的开源软件生态。此举可能影响政府采购、软件主权以及持续的现代化进程。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.mos.cms.futurecdn.net/iPvfpzYoJfo4RCLhXPfRAb-2000-80.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.mos.cms.futurecdn.net/iPvfpzYoJfo4RCLhXPfRAb-2000-80.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49897993' data-tags='Energy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(59) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.bbc.com/future/article/20260928-a-virtual-power-plant-hidden-in-vermont-homes-is-keeping-the-lights-on-during-storms' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Vermont replacing power plants with home batteries</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>佛蒙特州以家庭电池取代发电厂</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49897993' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 378</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49897993' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 295</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Vermont is replacing centralized power plants with a distributed network of home batteries forming a virtual power plant that keeps the lights on during outages. This approach shows how residential storage can stabilize grids and scale with adoption.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>佛蒙特州通过家庭电池组成分布式虚拟电厂，在停电时维持供电。此举展示了家庭储能如何稳定电网、并随普及度提升具备扩展性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://ychef.files.bbci.co.uk/624x351/p0pczkzt.jpg' data-full='https://ychef.files.bbci.co.uk/624x351/p0pczkzt.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49920932' data-tags='Business,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(60) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.techpowerup.com/353296/micron-ceo-says-memory-supply-will-be-much-tighter-in-2027-and-2028-than-in-2026' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Micron CEO Says Memory Supply Will Be Much Tighter in 2027 and 2028 Than in 2026</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美光称 2027-2028 年内存供给将显著比 2026 年更紧张</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49920932' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 378</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49920932' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 435</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Micron&#x27;s CEO warns memory supply will tighten in 2027-2028 more than in 2026, signaling supply-demand imbalance as AI workloads grow and memory demand rises. The forecast could influence pricing and capex planning for memory-intensive industries.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>美光首席执行官警告称，2027年至2028年的内存供给将比2026年更加紧张，随着 AI 工作负载增加对内存需求上升，市场可能出现供需失衡。这一预测或影响内存定价与高容量行业的资本支出规划。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.techpowerup.com/img/gh4AFBdm8kLj5cs8.jpg' data-full='https://www.techpowerup.com/img/gh4AFBdm8kLj5cs8.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49932147' data-tags='Space'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(61) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://bsky.app/profile/theplanetaryguy.com/post/3mwucf5ert22f' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A 12-year sequence of telescope images of a star and four planets orbiting</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>12 年序列的望远镜影像：一颗恒星及四颗行星的轨道</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49932147' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 378</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49932147' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 77</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A 12-year sequence of telescope images reveals orbital motion of a star and four planets, offering a rare long-term view of exoplanet dynamics and system stability.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一组长达 12 年的望远镜影像揭示了一颗恒星及其四颗行星的轨道运动，提供罕见的外星行星动力学与系统稳定性观测。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://video.bsky.app/watch/did%3Aplc%3Awwbktud2d34ont3frkv73pjo/bafkreigrgiobnhj6rl64sejfafxzpaife6lahnriedl6uc6mox5ebppmym/thumbnail.jpg' data-full='https://video.bsky.app/watch/did%3Aplc%3Awwbktud2d34ont3frkv73pjo/bafkreigrgiobnhj6rl64sejfafxzpaife6lahnriedl6uc6mox5ebppmym/thumbnail.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49880312' data-tags='Programming,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(62) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.ssp.sh/brain/the-problem-is-not-the-ai-code-but-nobody-knows-anything-anymore/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The problem is not AI code, but not knowing about system architecture or intent</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>问题不在AI代码，而在于对体系结构与意图的缺乏了解</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49880312' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 377</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49880312' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 238</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The real bottleneck isn’t the AI-generated code but humans’ lack of systemic context. Without solid architecture understanding and a clear intent, AI-assisted code can misfit, leading to brittle systems and governance gaps.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>真正的问题不是 AI 生成的代码，而是对系统架构、目标与上下文的缺乏清晰理解。缺乏架构层面的把握与明确意图，AI 产物容易不贴合需求，造成系统脆弱与治理漏洞。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.ssp.sh/brain/_img/feature/gen/the-problem-is-not-the-ai-code-but-nobody-knows-anything-anymore.webp' data-full='https://www.ssp.sh/brain/_img/feature/gen/the-problem-is-not-the-ai-code-but-nobody-knows-anything-anymore.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49854219' data-tags='Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(63) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mitxela.com/projects/flipflip' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Flip Fluid on Flip Dots</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在翻转点阵上实现流体效果</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49854219' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 376</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49854219' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 24</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A playful exploration of simulating fluid-like motion on a retro flip-dot display. The project maps fluid dynamics to the mechanical flipping of dots, showing how to achieve smooth animation within tight hardware limits and with clever firmware tricks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个对在复古翻转点阵上模拟流体般运动的有趣尝试。将流体动力学映射到点阵的机械翻转，展示在硬件极限与创意固件技巧下如何实现平滑动画。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://mitxela.com/img/titles/mitxela_dot_com-65.png' data-full='https://mitxela.com/img/titles/mitxela_dot_com-65.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49909583' data-tags='Finance,Business'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(64) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://spectrum.ieee.org/bloomberg-terminal' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A brief history of the Bloomberg terminal</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>彭博终端的简史</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49909583' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 374</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49909583' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 166</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Bloomberg Terminal revolutionized finance by bundling real-time market data, trading tools, and messaging into a single terminal. The piece traces its origins in the 1980s, its enduring business model, and how it shaped data licensing and professional workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>彭博终端自1980年代起就把实时市场数据、交易工具和通讯功能打包到一个设备中，彻底改变金融行业的工作流程与信息获取方式。文章回顾其发展历程、持久的商业模式，以及对数据授权和专业工作流的深远影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://spectrum.ieee.org/media-library/image.jpg?id=67857174&amp;width=1200&amp;height=600&amp;coordinates=0%2C50%2C0%2C50' data-full='https://spectrum.ieee.org/media-library/image.jpg?id=67857174&amp;width=1200&amp;height=600&amp;coordinates=0%2C50%2C0%2C50' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49867067' data-tags='Data,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(65) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://unsung.aresluna.org/they-had-no-concept-of-a-duty-of-care-to-their-users/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>On caring for user data: NeoVim caused Vim undo files to be deleted</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>关于对用户数据的关怀：NeoVim 导致 Vim 的撤销文件被删除</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49867067' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 369</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49867067' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 326</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A data-care incident shows NeoVim inadvertently deleting Vim undo files, prompting a reevaluation of cross-project data safety and the duty of care in software design. The piece argues for clearer expectations around user data preservation and compatibility.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一次数据关怀事件暴露 NeoVim 无意中删除了 Vim 的撤销文件，促使人们重新评估跨项目的数据安全和软件设计中的关怀义务。文章呼吁在用户数据保留与跨版本兼容方面设定更清晰的期望。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://unsung.aresluna.org/_media/they-had-no-concept-of-a-duty-of-care-to-their-users/ogimage.jpg' data-full='https://unsung.aresluna.org/_media/they-had-no-concept-of-a-duty-of-care-to-their-users/ogimage.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(66) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://stillwet.art/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Giving Opus 5.5 a simulated paint canvas</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：给 Opus 5.5 增添模拟绘画画布</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49928566' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 362</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49928566' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 108</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Show HN demonstrates giving Opus 5.5 a simulated paint canvas, illustrating how the model can emulate brushwork and texture in a browser-friendly setup. It&#x27;s a practical example of blending AI with artistic workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Show HN 展示如何为 Opus 5.5 增加一个模拟绘画画布，演示模型在浏览器环境中模拟笔触和纹理的能力。这是将 AI 与艺术工作流结合的实际案例。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49879000' data-tags='Business,DevOps,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(67) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.reuters.com/technology/mongodb-ceo-desai-steps-down-lead-metas-enterprise-platform-2026-09-28/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>MongoDB CEO resigns to join Meta</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>MongoDB 的 CEO 辞职加入 Meta</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49879000' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 357</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49879000' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 291</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The MongoDB CEO is stepping down to join Meta, signaling a leadership shift between database infrastructure and a major tech platform. The move could influence MongoDB’s strategy and underscores Meta’s push to strengthen its enterprise tooling and data stack.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>MongoDB 的首席执行官宣布辞职，加入 Meta，标志着数据库基础设施领域高层人才向大型科技平台的流动。此举可能影响 MongoDB 的战略方向，也反映 Meta 在企业级工具与数据栈方面的布局。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49946393' data-tags='Space,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(68) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://notoriousbfg.com/hole-punch/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Hole Punch: Sling your spaceship around gravitational fields</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Hole Punch：在引力场中甩动太空船的技巧</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49946393' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 354</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49946393' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 87</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This piece explores how Hole Punch leverages gravity wells to perform gravity-assist-like maneuvers, offering techniques for sling-shotting a spacecraft. It delves into the underlying orbital dynamics and potential implications for game design and physics simulations.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文解读 Hole Punch 如何利用引力井实现类似重力助推的轨迹绕行，揭示太空动力学中的技巧。内容涉及关键原理、潜在的优化点，以及此类机制对游戏设计和物理仿真的影响。</p>
