<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(69) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://dwarfstar.sh/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>From the creator of Redis; run LLM locally with ds4</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>来自 Redis 创始人之手；用 ds4 本地运行 LLM</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49936575' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 354</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49936575' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 100</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>From the creator of Redis, ds4 is a local LLM runner designed to execute inference on commodity hardware, avoiding cloud latency and preserving privacy. It targets efficient memory usage and practical offline experimentation with open models, lowering the barrier to private AI pilots.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>来自 Redis 创始人之手的 ds4 是一个本地大型语言模型推理工具，旨在在普通硬件上离线运行、避免云端延迟并提升隐私性。它强调高效的内存使用，降低了私有 AI 试验的门槛。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(70) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://fortune.com/2026/10/01/ai-godfather-yann-lecun-has-zero-concerns-about-human-extinction-says-anthropic-ceo-dario-amodei-is-deuded/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>LeCun has &quot;zero concerns&quot; about AI wiping out humanity, recent &quot;rogue&quot; incidents</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>LeCun 对 AI 不会消灭人类的观点：零担忧</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49946228' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 353</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49946228' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 642</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Yann LeCun publicly says he has zero concerns about AI wiping out humanity, arguing recent incidents don&#x27;t justify existential fear. The remarks illustrate the ongoing debate between industry optimism and safety advocates about AI&#x27;s trajectory.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Yann LeCun 公共场合表示对 AI 造成的人类灭绝风险“零担忧”，称近期事件并不足以改变这一判断。这反映出业界乐观与安全倡导者之间的持续辩论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://fortune.com/img-assets/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/GettyImages-2197497048-e1790790386809.jpg?resize=1200,600' data-full='https://fortune.com/img-assets/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/GettyImages-2197497048-e1790790386809.jpg?resize=1200,600' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(71) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.osnews.com/story/146052/google-breaks-promise-to-provide-10-years-of-updates-to-chromebooks/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Google breaks promise to provide 10 years of updates to Chromebooks</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>谷歌未兑现 Chromebook 的 10 年更新承诺</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49920997' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 351</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49920997' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 156</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Google appears to be backing away from its pledge of 10 years of software updates for Chromebooks, potentially shortening the support window for many devices. The move could disrupt long-life deployment plans in schools and businesses and raise security and depreciation concerns as devices age.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>谷歌似乎正在削减对 Chromebook 10 年更新承诺的落实，可能缩短许多设备的支持周期。这一举动或将扰乱学校和企业的长期部署计划，并在设备老化时带来安全与折旧方面的担忧。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(72) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://david.alvarezrosa.com/posts/self-hosting-on-the-dark-web/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Self-Hosting on the Dark Web</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在暗网自托管</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Explores how to run self-hosted services on the dark web, including onion services, encryption, and threat modeling. It weighs privacy benefits against performance constraints and legal risks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>介绍在暗网实现自托管服务的要点，包括 Onion 服务、加密和威胁建模。讨论隐私收益与性能限制以及潜在法律风险的权衡。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49926411' data-tags='Science,Health,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(73) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://erictopol.substack.com/p/loss-of-cell-identity-drives-human' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Loss of cell identity drives human aging: Two new papers</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>细胞身份丧失推动人类衰老：两篇新论文</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49926411' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 350</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49926411' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 166</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Two papers argue that loss of cell identity drives aging, with cells gradually losing their specialized programs. Reasserting or reprogramming cell identity could open new anti-aging strategies that go beyond traditional longevity approaches.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>两篇论文认为，细胞身份的丧失推动衰老，细胞逐渐偏离特定组织的专门化程序。重建或重新编程细胞身份可能开启新的抗衧老策略，超越传统的延缓寿命方法。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(74) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://jagi.studio/posts/phyllotaxis/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Phyllotaxis: An audio-reactive LED display</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49880411' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 344</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49880411' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 50</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A project that turns audio input into evolving LED patterns using a phyllotaxis-inspired layout. It demonstrates real-time generative visuals and a tactile approach to audio-reactive art, bridging nature-inspired math with hardware creativity.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个把音频输入实时转化为 LED 动态图案的作品，采用花序结构的布局。展示了将自然启发的几何与音频驱动的可视化相结合的即时生成艺术。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49945933' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(75) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://liao.gg/blog/agents-dont-need-memory' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Agents don&#x27;t need memory, they need documentation</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>代理不需要记忆，他们需要文档</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49945933' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 344</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49945933' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 210</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A counterpoint to memory-augmented agents, arguing that structured, queryable documentation can be more reliable than raw memory for AI systems. The piece advocates embedding knowledge in accessible artifacts to improve reasoning and auditability in AI systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>提出代理人不需要大规模记忆，而需要结构化、可查询的文档。文章建议将知识嵌入可访问的资料中，以提升可靠性、可审计性和可解释性。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49927747' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 338</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49927747' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 324</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>ChatGPT adds a &#x27;Sites&#x27; feature to publish and share pages built from chat interactions, streamlining content sharing without leaving the chat. This tight coupling of creation and publishing suits knowledge work and rapid demos.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>ChatGPT 新增“Sites”功能，可以直接从对话中创建并发布网页，简化内容分享流程。将创作与发布紧密结合，适合知识工作和快速演示。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(77) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/ntfargo/Relapse-Exploit' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>PS5 Relapse Exploit</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>PS5 Relapse 漏洞利用</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49895304' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 336</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49895304' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 226</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Relapse appears to be a PS5 vulnerability exploit that enables arbitrary code execution on affected firmware, potentially allowing homebrew or custom firmware. The discovery underscores persistent security risks in console ecosystems and the ongoing race between protection and exploitation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Relapse 漏洞利用工具可能在受影响的固件上实现任意代码执行，或允许自制固件和自制程序。此发现再次凸显游戏主机生态系统的长期安全风险，以及防护与利用之间的博弈。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/7fe675eade5fc5efb84b740dc30420d5254c06b7023856e0cc8cc23d7015cb2d/ntfargo/Relapse-Exploit' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/7fe675eade5fc5efb84b740dc30420d5254c06b7023856e0cc8cc23d7015cb2d/ntfargo/Relapse-Exploit' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49917761' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 321</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49917761' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 137</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A provocative post voices strong opposition to Google&#x27;s Android Developer Verification Program, arguing the policy creates friction for developers and raises concerns about privacy and gatekeeping. The discussion highlights ongoing tensions between platform control and developer-friendly onboarding.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这条观点明确表达对 Android 开发者验证计划的强烈不满，认为该计划增加了开发者的门槛并引发隐私与平台把关等担忧。讨论反映出平台控制与开发者体验之间的持续张力。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(79) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://git.mills.io/prologic/parley' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Parley: Federated, decentralised chat that speaks plain IRC</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Parley：联邦化、去中心化的聊天，像 IRC 一样简单</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49875913' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 321</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49875913' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 187</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Parley federates chat across servers using an IRC-like protocol, offering a decentralized alternative to centralized chat apps. It emphasizes user ownership and interoperability over platform-lock-in. Adoption will depend on ecosystem tooling and client support.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Parley 将聊天跨服务器联邦化，使用类 IRC 的协议，提供一个去中心化的替代方案。它强调用户掌控和互操作性，而非被单一平台锁定。能否被广泛采用取决于生态工具和客户端支持的成熟度。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://git.mills.io/repo-avatars/640d1c8d0740a84bc305d7d7f83dc71164e891e05a7ac78a26d5199d985d7cd1' data-full='https://git.mills.io/repo-avatars/640d1c8d0740a84bc305d7d7f83dc71164e891e05a7ac78a26d5199d985d7cd1' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49929391' data-tags='Security,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(80) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=NnV_cWeoo5Q' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Greg Kroah-Hartman – Security in the LLM Age [video]</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Greg Kroah-Hartman：LLM时代的安全性 [视频]</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49929391' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 317</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49929391' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 115</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Greg Kroah-Hartman discusses security in the LLM age, highlighting new risks from large models, prompts, and supply chains. He argues for stronger containment, safer interfaces, and better tooling for kernel and system security as AI becomes integral to software. The talk offers practical takeaways for developers building AI-assisted systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Greg Kroah-Hartman 讨论了在 LLM 时代的安全挑战，聚焦大模型、提示攻击和供应链等新风险。他主张加强容器化与隔离、改进接口安全，以及在内核与系统层提供更好的安全工具。此视频为构建 AI 助力系统的开发者提供了切实可行的建议。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(82) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://manuel.darcemont.fr/posts/the-last-time-my-family-was-replaced-by-technology/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The last time my family was replaced by technology</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>科技替代家人：我的家人经历的那一次</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49908394' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 311</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49908394' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 608</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A personal reflection on how technology reshaped family life, perhaps displacing in-person interactions with screens. It explores balancing tech use with meaningful togetherness and the bittersweet costs of always-on connectivity.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对科技如何改变家庭生活的个人反思，描述屏幕替代了面对面互动的场景。探讨在始终在线的便利与陪伴质量之间寻找平衡，以及数字化带来的成本。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49946355' data-tags='Legal'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(83) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://cosmowenman.substack.com/p/rodin-museum-3d-scan-verdict' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Treachery in the Rodin Museum 3D scan verdict</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>罗丹博物馆3D扫描案裁决</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49946355' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 310</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49946355' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 185</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A court verdict in a Rodin Museum 3D scanning case clarifies how such scans are treated under copyright and licensing. The decision could shape ownership of digital replicas and set a precedent for museums pursuing or restricting digitization and commercial use.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>裁决阐明对艺术品数字化扫描在版权与许可方面的处理方式，将影响数字复制品的所有权，以及博物馆在数字化与商业化方面的决策。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49937631' data-tags='Privacy,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(84) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://developer.apple.com/news/?id=p6zjojqw' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Updates to Full Disk Access in macOS</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>macOS 全磁盘访问权限更新</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49937631' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 307</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49937631' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 216</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Apple updates macOS full disk access permissions, adding more granular prompts and directory-scoped controls. The changes push developers toward transparent data usage and safer defaults, reshaping how apps request broad file-system access.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>苹果对 macOS 的全磁盘访问权限进行了更新，增加了更细粒度的提示和基于目录的控制。改动促使开发者在数据使用上更透明、默认设置更安全，从而重塑应用请求广域文件系统访问的方式。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://developer.apple.com/news/images/og/full-disk-access-og.png' data-full='https://developer.apple.com/news/images/og/full-disk-access-og.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(85) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.thespacereview.com/article/4951/1' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The top secret URSALA, RAQUEL, and FARRAH satellites (2025)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>绝密卫星：URSALA、RAQUEL 与 FARRAH</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49915082' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 306</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49915082' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 162</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Explores the rumored trio of top-secret satellites codenamed URSALA, RAQUEL, and FARRAH, piecing together what little public metadata can imply about their missions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>探讨被传闻为绝密的三颗卫星，代号为 URSALA、RAQUEL 与 FARRAH，并结合公开数据尝试推断它们的任务。强调隐蔽资产对跟踪与信息解密带来的复杂性，以及现有轨道数据空白对其存在与用途的暗示。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.thespacereview.com/images/logo-tsr-1.gif' data-full='https://www.thespacereview.com/images/logo-tsr-1.gif' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-title'>(86) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.worldlabs.ai/blog/amd-announcement' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>World Labs is Joining AMD</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>World Labs 将加入 AMD 生态</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49883760' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 305</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49883760' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 120</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>World Labs announces joining AMD&#x27;s ecosystem to leverage AMD&#x27;s AI accelerators and GPUs for its workloads. The move signals ongoing consolidation as AI infrastructure players align with major hardware vendors.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>World Labs 宣布加入 AMD 生态，计划利用 AMD 的 AI 加速器与显卡来支撑其工作负载。此举反映出 AI 基础设施领域与主要硬件厂商持续的整合趋势。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(87) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/omlahore/RemoveMacAI' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Turn off Apple Intelligence on macOS 27 and get its disk space back</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在 macOS 27 上关闭 Apple Intelligence 以释放磁盘空间</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49957116' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 305</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49957116' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 189</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>macOS 27&#x27;s AI features can consume significant disk space through caches and data. A community-made guide shows how to disable Apple Intelligence to reclaim storage, with a trade-off in AI-assisted functionality.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>macOS 27 的 Apple Intelligence 功能会占用磁盘空间。通过关闭这一功能，可以释放存储，但可能会牺牲部分 AI 辅助功能。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://repository-images.githubusercontent.com/1394989252/13dcf083-d511-4f51-9681-c6ccbda3cae9' data-full='https://repository-images.githubusercontent.com/1394989252/13dcf083-d511-4f51-9681-c6ccbda3cae9' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49875411' data-tags='Space'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(88) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.space.com/space-exploration/launches-spacecraft/spacexs-starship-megarocket-launching-to-orbit-for-1st-time-ever-on-sept-28-watch-it-live' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>SpaceX&#x27;s Starship launching to orbit for first time ever today</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>SpaceX 的 Starship 首次进入轨道发射</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49875411' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 303</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49875411' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 360</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Starship&#x27;s first orbital flight marks a landmark for fully reusable space launch tech, testing end-to-end performance from fueling to recovery. Success or failure will influence future satellite deployment, lunar and Mars ambitions, and investor sentiment.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>SpaceX 的 Starship 首次进入轨道发射标志着可完全重复使用的太空发射技术的里程碑，涵盖从加注到回收的端到端测试。成败都将影响未来卫星部署、月球与火星任务的前景，以及投资者情绪。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.mos.cms.futurecdn.net/dfLan3EoZiwtAAjvoSR8KK-2560-80.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.mos.cms.futurecdn.net/dfLan3EoZiwtAAjvoSR8KK-2560-80.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49883970' data-tags='Security,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(89) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.faav.net/how-i-couldve-accessed-17-trillion-microsoft-records' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>I could&#x27;ve accessed 17T Microsoft records</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我本来可以访问17万亿条微软记录</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49883970' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 302</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49883970' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 124</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A disclosure-style post argues that misconfigured access points could expose an enormous trove of Microsoft records, underscoring the fragility of giant data stores. It calls for stronger access controls, auditing, and responsible disclosure practices.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者声称通过某些错误配置可以访问17万亿条微软记录，凸显巨型数据仓库的脆弱性。文章强调需要更强的访问控制、更完善的审计与负责任的披露流程。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blog.faav.net/assets/images/647380bf-4a6a-462c-b2f6-f16f2f02bc19.png' data-full='https://blog.faav.net/assets/images/647380bf-4a6a-462c-b2f6-f16f2f02bc19.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49896712' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(90) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.tcl-lang.org/software/tcltk/9.1.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Tcl/Tk 9.1</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Tcl/Tk 9.1 发布</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49896712' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 301</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49896712' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 143</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Tcl/Tk 9.1 brings incremental improvements to both the scripting language and the Tk GUI toolkit, focusing on stability and cross-platform consistency. Developers can expect better packaging, refreshed widgets, and smoother interoperability with modern runtimes. The update reinforces Tcl/Tk’s role for lightweight, scriptable desktop UIs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Tcl/Tk 9.1 在脚本语言与 Tk GUI 工具包上带来渐进改进，重点提升稳定性和跨平台一致性。开发者将享受更好的打包、更新的控件以及与现代运行时的更好互操作性。更新强化了 Tcl/Tk 在轻量化、可脚本化桌面 UI 领域的地位。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(91) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://gwern.net/doc/math/1973-halmos.pdf' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Legend of von Neumann (1973) [pdf]</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49933235' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 300</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49933235' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 174</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A concise retrospective essay revisits the legend of John von Neumann, separating folklore from reality. It surveys his wide-ranging contributions to mathematics and computing and reflects on how his persona helped shape the way we think about computation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇简短的回顾性随笔区分了冯·诺依曼传说与真实贡献，梳理他在数学与计算领域的广泛贡献，并反思他的形象如何塑造了人们对计算的认知。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(101) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.cloudflare.com/cloudflare-k2-streams/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Cloudflare K2: serverless event streams</a></p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Seven tiny LLMs run directly in the browser, showcasing lightweight, client-side AI options that don’t require server calls.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在浏览器中直接运行七个微型大语言模型，展示无需服务器就能用的轻量级AI选项。强调浏览器原生AI演示的实用性，以及小模型在性能和用户体验方面的权衡。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(103) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://postalemployeenetwork.com/news/2026/09/26/u-s-postal-inspectors-shut-down-website-selling-millions-of-counterfeit-postage-labels/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>U.S. postal inspectors shut down website selling counterfeit postage labels</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美国邮政检查人员关闭销售假冒邮资标签的网站</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49899090' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 277</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49899090' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 168</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>U.S. postal inspectors shut down a website selling counterfeit postage labels, likely ending a supply chain that could enable mail fraud on a large scale. The action underscores ongoing fraud risks in logistics and the importance of enforcement across digital storefronts. This case illustrates how counterfeit materials can affect revenue and trust in postal services.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>美国邮政检查人员关闭了一个销售假冒邮资标签的网站，打断了可能用于大规模邮件欺诈的供应链。此举凸显物流领域持续存在的欺诈风险，以及对数字商店加强执法的必要性。此事揭示了假标签如何影响邮政服务的收入与信任。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(104) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.cbc.ca/lite/story/9.7361622' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>1 in 8 cancer cases worldwide are caused by infections, study finds</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>全球癌症的感染性成因占比约1/8的研究</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49892120' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 277</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49892120' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 146</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A global burden study finds infections account for about 12.5% of cancers, underscoring the role of vaccination and infection control in cancer prevention.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究发现全球约 1/8 的癌症与感染相关，强调疫苗接种和感染控制在癌症预防中的作用。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(105) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.404media.co/cops-can-bypass-iphone-automatic-inactivity-reboot-graykey/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Cops Can Bypass iPhone&#x27;s Automatic Reboot to Get into Locked Phones</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49922278' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 275</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49922278' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 218</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>New findings show law enforcement can bypass iPhone&#x27;s automatic reboot to access locked devices, highlighting ongoing tensions between device encryption and investigative access. The development could influence future policy debates and forensic tooling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>新发现表明执法部门可以绕过 iPhone 的自动重启机制进入锁定设备，凸显了设备端到端加密与取证访问之间的持续博弈。这一进展可能影响未来的政策辩论和取证工具的发展。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(106) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.petervijeh.com/projects/reddit-astroturf' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Does Reddit have an astroturfing problem? What the data suggests</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Reddit 是否存在刷量造势问题？数据给出的启示</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49877678' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 275</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49877678' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 359</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Data suggests Reddit hosts coordinated manipulation campaigns across topics; patterns include timing, cross-subreddit activity, and bot-like behavior. The findings highlight challenges in detecting astroturfing and reputational risk for communities.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>数据指出 Reddit 上存在协同行动的操纵化宣传现象，表现为跨子版块的时间安排、协同互动和机器人化行为等模式。这揭示了识别刷量造势的难点及对社区声誉的潜在风险。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://petervijeh.com/projects/reddit-astroturf/opengraph-image?ea42a52299b9e000' data-full='https://petervijeh.com/projects/reddit-astroturf/opengraph-image?ea42a52299b9e000' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(107) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arstechnica.com/science/2026/10/ai-finally-beat-the-best-stratego-player-in-history-and-did-it-on-a-budget/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>With most information hidden, the game Stratego had stumped AI until now</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>信息几乎全被隐藏的 Stratego 一直让 AI 无法取胜，如今首次被击败</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49933740' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 275</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49933740' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 139</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AI had struggled with Stratego because most information is hidden, creating a challenging imperfect-information setting. A new approach (and a tight compute budget) finally defeats top human players in this game. The result advances planning under uncertainty and shows progress in AI&#x27;s ability to handle hidden information.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>由于 Stratego 的信息高度隐藏，AI 长期难以取胜，属于不完美信息环境的挑战。最新方法在有限预算下首次击败顶尖人类选手，推动在不确定性情境下的规划能力。此进展凸显 AI 在处理隐含信息方面的新突破。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.arstechnica.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/10/GettyImages-1864707238-1152x648.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.arstechnica.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/10/GettyImages-1864707238-1152x648.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(108) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://nolanlawson.com/2026/10/03/why-dont-more-developers-use-the-platform/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why don&#x27;t more developers “use the platform”?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>为何没有更多开发者真正使用这个平台？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49950554' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 272</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49950554' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 287</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Developers often hesitate to use a platform despite its promises, due to fragmentation, weak tooling, or unclear incentives. The piece argues for a better developer experience, concrete migration paths, and compelling value to raise adoption.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>尽管平台具备潜力，开发者往往因为生态碎片化、工具不足或激励不足而不愿真正采用。文章提出应改善开发者体验、提供清晰的迁移路径，并创造更具吸引力的价值以提升接受度。</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文给出在 2026 年 9 月提升 rustc 速度的要点，强调增量构建、缓存和选择性代码生成等实用优化，帮助大型 Rust 项目缩短编译时间。</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一位 Webster 男子为母亲在他少年时捐献的肾脏庆祝百岁生日，体现器官移植的长期生存能力。报道还反映了捐献者肾脏在几十年内仍能正常工作的现实，以及移植医疗的持续进展。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(114) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theguardian.com/technology/2026/oct/03/openai-safety-leader-quits-warning-ai-companys-culture-is-broken' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>OpenAI safety leader quits, warning AI company&#x27;s culture is &#x27;broken&#x27;</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OpenAI 安全负责人离职，警告公司文化已‘破碎’</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49948332' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 265</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49948332' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 3</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI&#x27;s head of safety has resigned, saying the company culture undervalues safety work and promotes rushing products. The departure underscores tensions between rapid feature release and risk management at leading AI labs, with potential implications for governance, transparency, and regulatory approaches.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenAI 的安全负责人已离职，表示公司文化低估安全工作、推动仓促上线产品。此事凸显在全球领先的 AI 研究机构中，快速迭代与风险管理之间的张力，可能对治理、透明度及监管路径产生影响。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Presents a practical framework to measure and improve factory quality in Factorio, with concrete patterns to identify bottlenecks and waste. It emphasizes modular design, throughput benchmarks, and robust error handling in factory layouts.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文提出在 Factorio 中评估并提升产线质量的实用框架，给出可操作的设计模式与可量化指标，帮助玩家定位瓶颈与浪费。通过模块化设计和吞吐基准，提升大规模工厂的鲁棒性和效率。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49866951' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 264</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49866951' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 97</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers report a surprising experiment conducted in a modest motel room that sheds light on plausible paths to the origin of life. The setup emphasizes how simple chemical steps could assemble into complex, self-sustaining systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究在一个普通的旅馆房间里完成的实验，为生命起源提供了新的线索。结果强调了简单化学步骤如何聚合成更复杂、具自我维持能力的系统。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(119) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://lecaro.me/20260921-google-less.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>So long Google, and thanks for all the nudes</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>再见谷歌，感谢你带来所有裸体照</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49881951' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 261</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49881951' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 107</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece provocatively critiques Google&#x27;s handling of nude imagery and related privacy/content policies, arguing that platform decisions around sensitive media reflect broader tensions between user privacy and platform-scale data processing. It frames the shift as part of a larger trend toward more privacy-conscious content tooling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章以挑衅口吻讨论谷歌在处理裸体图像及相关隐私与内容政策方面的取舍，指出平台在敏感媒体处理上的决策折射出用户隐私与平台规模化数据处理之间的博弈。并将这一转变置于日益注重隐私保护的内容工具趋势之中。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49867486' data-tags='Culture,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(120) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.ihatethefuture.com/2026/09/the-normalization-of-inexplicable.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Normalization of Inexplicable Failures</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>不可解释的故障日常化</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49867486' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 261</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49867486' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 107</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that tech culture has normalized unexplained outages and flaky behavior, eroding trust. It calls for better postmortems, clearer failure signals, and more resilience engineering.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章指出科技行业对不可解释的故障已形成常态，对信任造成侵蚀。呼吁更透明的事后分析、明确的故障信号和更强的韧性工程。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blogger.googleusercontent.com/img/b/R29vZ2xl/AVvXsEgWqOvF787cVQ7sqEe9MDHTU-hSCIot6pf5UrOqxmfAXhrwqcZvIIN4JWF9sQC5DYQ1qb7_9Ok7XuZGulQ9XH2sLSrjtwqGgJmP_jmzRDPJUBtnxolAij6heLf2oXP7sAHCG0aczxQzBV185kXeZbKvdvtz4_5Ext6EJvTpIr4hKA4pQJKhEp-Mg40H-w0/w1200-h630-p-k-no-nu/curtis_stupid-thing-sucks_1_door-blog.gif' data-full='https://blogger.googleusercontent.com/img/b/R29vZ2xl/AVvXsEgWqOvF787cVQ7sqEe9MDHTU-hSCIot6pf5UrOqxmfAXhrwqcZvIIN4JWF9sQC5DYQ1qb7_9Ok7XuZGulQ9XH2sLSrjtwqGgJmP_jmzRDPJUBtnxolAij6heLf2oXP7sAHCG0aczxQzBV185kXeZbKvdvtz4_5Ext6EJvTpIr4hKA4pQJKhEp-Mg40H-w0/w1200-h630-p-k-no-nu/curtis_stupid-thing-sucks_1_door-blog.gif' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49906100' data-tags='AI,Programming,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(121) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/maanHimself/OpenDLSS-NR' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>OpenDLSS: A Vulkan Reimplementation of Nvidia&#x27;s DLSS 5 Neural Rendering Network</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OpenDLSS-NR：基于 Vulkan 的 Nvidia DLSS 5 神经渲染网络实现</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49906100' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 258</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49906100' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 119</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OpenDLSS-NR is a Vulkan-based open-source reimplementation of Nvidia&#x27;s DLSS 5 neural rendering network, aiming to bring similar upscaling quality to a wider set of GPUs. If successful, it could empower developers to integrate neural upscaling into Vulkan apps without relying on Nvidia&#x27;s stack, but performance and fidelity benchmarks remain uncertain.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OpenDLSS-NR 是基于 Vulkan 的 Nvidia DLSS 5 神经渲染网络的开源实现，目标是在更广泛的硬件上提供类似的超分辨能力。若成熟可让开发者在 Vulkan 应用中实现神经上采样，而不必依赖 Nvidia 的专有栈，但性能和画质的基准仍在发展中。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/7afbcbfe098a0d39a07d308edad004db1d3bdef7387697070ba80cf6ae6c446d/maanHimself/OpenDLSS-NR' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/7afbcbfe098a0d39a07d308edad004db1d3bdef7387697070ba80cf6ae6c446d/maanHimself/OpenDLSS-NR' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49933869' data-tags='Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(122) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://yuka.dev/blog-2026-10-02-linux-m4.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Forgetful CPU (Linux on M4)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>忘记的 CPU：在 M4 上运行 Linux</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49933869' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 253</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49933869' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 184</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A developer explores running Linux on an M4-class MCU, detailing the hardware constraints and the tricks needed to boot and operate a Linux userland on such a small, memory-limited device.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者尝试在 M4 级微控制器上运行 Linux，聚焦缺乏 MMU、内存有限等硬件约束，以及在如此受限设备上实现 Linux 的关键技巧。这一尝试揭示了嵌入式 Linux 的新边界，以及微控制器层面上运行复杂软件的潜在可能。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://yuka.dev/files/sven-m4-sptm.png' data-full='https://yuka.dev/files/sven-m4-sptm.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49921118' data-tags='Business,AI,DevOps,Energy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(123) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nytimes.com/2026/09/30/technology/meta-ai-data-centers-taxes.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Meta Uses A.I. Data Centers to Avoid Billions in Federal Taxes</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Meta 使用 AI 数据中心规避数十亿美元联邦税</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49921118' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 252</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49921118' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 242</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Meta reportedly uses AI-focused data centers as part of tax planning to reduce its federal tax bill by billions. The strategy highlights how large tech firms leverage capital investments and energy deductions around data-center infrastructure to lower taxes, prompting scrutiny from lawmakers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据报道，Meta 通过投资于 AI 数据中心进行税务筹划，以大幅降低联邦税负。这一策略揭示大型科技公司如何利用数据中心相关投资与能源抵扣来减税，引发立法者的关注与审查。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49912955' data-tags='Science,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(124) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.quantamagazine.org/surprisingly-complex-waves-reveal-the-brains-inner-workings-20260930/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Surprisingly complex waves reveal the brain&#x27;s inner workings</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>令人惊讶地复杂的脑波揭示大脑内部运作</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49912955' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 251</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49912955' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 96</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>New measurements reveal brain activity exhibits surprisingly complex, multi-scale waves, challenging simple models of neural function. The finding reshapes how we model cognition, interpret neuroimaging, and design AI inspired by brain dynamics.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>新测量揭示大脑活动呈现出出乎意料的复杂、多尺度波动，挑战了简单的神经功能模型。这一发现将影响对认知建模、神经影像解读，以及受脑动力学启发的 AI 设计。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.quantamagazine.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/Thought-Geometry-cr.Mark-Belan-Social.jpg' data-full='https://www.quantamagazine.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/Thought-Geometry-cr.Mark-Belan-Social.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49926628' data-tags='Privacy,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(125) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://automatictransmission.khoury.northeastern.edu/index.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Automatic Transmission – a data-privacy study of connected vehicles</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>自动传输：连网车辆的数据隐私研究</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49926628' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 250</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49926628' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 216</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers analyze data flows from connected vehicles, highlighting privacy risks in telemetry, location data, and third-party sharing. The study points to potential exposure during maintenance, app integrations, and fleet operations. It also suggests mitigations like data minimization, on-device processing, and transparent consent in automotive ecosystems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究者对车联网中的数据流进行分析，揭示遥测、位置信息和第三方数据共享等隐私风险。研究还指出在维修、应用程序集成和车队运营等环节可能暴露数据。提出的缓解措施包括数据最小化、本地处理以及在汽车生态中实现透明的同意机制。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://automatictransmission.khoury.northeastern.edu/images/test-facility.png' data-full='https://automatictransmission.khoury.northeastern.edu/images/test-facility.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49897075' data-tags='AI,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(126) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.anthropic.com/research/glm-5-3-and-the-spread-of-advanced-cyber-capabilities' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>GLM-5.3 and the spread of advanced cyber capabilities</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GLM-5.3与高级网络能力的扩散</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49897075' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 249</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49897075' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 233</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>GLM-5.3 accelerates the spread of powerful cyber capabilities by lowering access barriers to advanced AI tools. This democratization can boost security research but also enable misuse, underscoring the need for safety controls, auditing, and responsible deployment.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>GLM-5.3降低了获取强大网络能力的门槛，促使先进的网络能力更广泛地传播。该研究揭示这种“民主化”可能带来安全研究机会，也可能放大滥用风险，强调需要更严格的安全控制、审计与负责任的部署。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www-cdn.anthropic.com/images/4zrzovbb/website/6d4a0d28992ade92d6fa63646fd9c9d318245c6c-2400x1260.jpg' data-full='https://www-cdn.anthropic.com/images/4zrzovbb/website/6d4a0d28992ade92d6fa63646fd9c9d318245c6c-2400x1260.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49913192' data-tags='Programming,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(127) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://edgcpp.org/#transition' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>EDG C++ front-end goes public</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>EDG C++ 前端公开发布</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49913192' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 248</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49913192' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 128</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>EDG&#x27;s C++ front-end has been made public, opening access to mature parsing and semantic-analysis tooling. The release could influence compiler development and language tooling, lowering barriers for research and experimentation with C++ front-end technology.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>EDG 的 C++ 前端公开发布，向开发者开放了成熟的语法分析与语义分析工具链。这一举动可能改变编译器工具生态，降低研究与试验 C++ 前端技术的门槛。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://edgcpp.org/assets/img/logo.gif' data-full='https://edgcpp.org/assets/img/logo.gif' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(128) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://gadgets.muse.ai' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Muse Gadgets</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Muse Gadgets</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49937504' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 247</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49937504' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 109</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Muse Gadgets showcases a lineup of modern hardware with a focus on thoughtful design and practical specs. The coverage hints at how AI-enabled gadgets are reshaping consumer tech and daily workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Muse Gadgets 展示了一批现代硬件，强调设计与实用规格。文章/评测暗示了 AI 助力的设备如何改变普通用户的日常工作方式。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://gadgets.muse.ai/gadgets/og/social.png' data-full='https://gadgets.muse.ai/gadgets/og/social.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49929113' data-tags='Privacy,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(129) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.singapore-samizdat.com/p/how-singapores-government-run-dating-service-firstdate-works' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>How Singapore&#x27;s government-run dating service works</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>新加坡政府运营的约会服务如何运作</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49929113' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 247</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49929113' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 345</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Singapore operates a government-run dating service that pairs individuals through a formal process, with screening and privacy protections. The program aims to surface compatible matches while safeguarding user data, but it also raises questions about privacy, inclusivity, and consent at scale.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>新加坡的政府运营的约会服务通过正式流程为个人搭配伴侣，并设有筛选和隐私保护措施。与其他公共项目类似，旨在在符合兼容性标准的前提下帮助配对，但也引发关于隐私、包容性和同意等方面的讨论。该文章还探讨了公共约会项目在效率、伦理与社会影响之间的取舍。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!g2hS!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Fa6da96f2-5327-4b47-9832-9e9bf0ee3093_2400x1860.png' data-full='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!g2hS!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Fa6da96f2-5327-4b47-9832-9e9bf0ee3093_2400x1860.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49891290' data-tags='AI,Programming,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(130) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/PostHog/jeeves' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Jeeves. Reasoning improves Jev-like decision models</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Jeeves：推理能力提升 Jev 风格决策模型</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49891290' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 239</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49891290' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 93</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Jeeves introduces explicit reasoning to Jev-like decision models, boosting reliability and interpretability. The project experiments with integrating step-by-step logic into AI-assisted analytics to improve decision quality.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Jeeves 将显式推理引入 Jev 风格的决策模型，提升可靠性与可解释性。该项目尝试将逐步推理逻辑嵌入 AI 辅助分析中，以提高决策质量。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/5899be754d0593da83a185505b0dbb8be8f69b17b6c618d60cc9751084bb768f/PostHog/jeeves' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/5899be754d0593da83a185505b0dbb8be8f69b17b6c618d60cc9751084bb768f/PostHog/jeeves' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49927100' data-tags='Education,Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(131) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://molily.de/web-dev-education/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The death of web development education</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>the death of web development education</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49927100' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 234</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49927100' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 190</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Web development education is failing to prepare students for today&#x27;s fast-changing frontend and backend stacks. The piece argues that traditional curricula are outdated, and suggests focusing on hands-on project experience, real-world workflows, and ongoing, self-directed learning rather than rote theory.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>当今前端与后端生态快速变化，传统的网页开发教育未能有效准备学生。文章主张摒弃落后的课程理论，转向基于项目的实战、真实工作流，以及持续的自学能力培养。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://molily.de/img/spidermum-gray-bg-square-small.png' data-full='https://molily.de/img/spidermum-gray-bg-square-small.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49912444' data-tags='DevOps,Web,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(132) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.netlify.com/blog/edge-functions-firecracker-microvms/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>5x faster Edge Functions: V8 isolates to Firecracker MicroVMs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>5倍加速的边缘函数：从 V8 isolates 到 Firecracker 微VM</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49912444' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 228</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49912444' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 104</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Netlify’s edge runtime shift uses Firecracker microVMs to isolate functions, delivering roughly 5x faster cold-starts and improved security compared with V8 isolates. The move signals a broader trend toward stronger isolation at the edge without sacrificing latency.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Netlify 将边缘函数从 V8 isolates 迁移到 Firecracker 微VM，带来约 5 倍的冷启动加速与更好的隔离性。此举反映了在边缘计算中以更强隔离换取低延迟的一股趋势。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.netlify.com/images/blog/edge-functions-firecracker-microvms.png' data-full='https://www.netlify.com/images/blog/edge-functions-firecracker-microvms.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49935867' data-tags='Politics,Legal'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(133) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.npr.org/2026/10/01/nx-s1-5977815/trump-icc-sanctions-kimberly-prost' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>ICC judge on what U.S. sanctions mean for her and global courts</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>ICC法官谈美国制裁对她及全球法院的影响</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49935867' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 228</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49935867' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 171</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Sanctions complicate her day-to-day work at the ICC, restricting communications and cooperation with partners and funders. The move raises concerns about judicial independence and the ability of international courts to pursue powerful actors.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这位法官解释了美国制裁对她本人及国际刑事法院日常工作的影响，特别是对与合作伙伴和资金来源的沟通与协作造成约束。此举引发对司法独立性及全球法院追究强势行为能力的广泛担忧。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://npr.brightspotcdn.com/dims3/default/strip/false/crop/5250x2953+0+662/resize/1400/quality/85/format/jpeg/?url=http%3A%2F%2Fnpr-brightspot.s3.amazonaws.com%2F00%2F8a%2F18a07f2b4b03ad943ddbb3cb4b52%2Fgettyimages-2296121876.jpg' data-full='https://npr.brightspotcdn.com/dims3/default/strip/false/crop/5250x2953+0+662/resize/1400/quality/85/format/jpeg/?url=http%3A%2F%2Fnpr-brightspot.s3.amazonaws.com%2F00%2F8a%2F18a07f2b4b03ad943ddbb3cb4b52%2Fgettyimages-2296121876.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49909056' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(134) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://buttondown.com/hillelwayne/archive/what-tla-can-and-cant-check/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>What TLA+ can and can&#x27;t check</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>TLA+ 能检查什么、不能检查什么</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49909056' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 227</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49909056' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 47</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>TLA+ excels at modeling concurrent systems and proving invariants, but it can&#x27;t verify all real-world properties that depend on timing, performance, or external systems. The piece underscores the discipline&#x27;s limits and best practices for formal verification.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>TLA+ 在建模并发系统、证明不变性方面很强，但对依赖时间、性能或外部系统的现实属性并非全能。文章强调了形式化验证的边界与最佳实践。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://image-generator.buttondown.email/api/emphasize-subject?subject=What%20TLA%2B%20can%20and%20can%27t%20check&amp;author=Computer%20Things&amp;date=2026-09-30&amp;img=' data-full='https://image-generator.buttondown.email/api/emphasize-subject?subject=What%20TLA%2B%20can%20and%20can%27t%20check&amp;author=Computer%20Things&amp;date=2026-09-30&amp;img=' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49926917' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 222</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49926917' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 78</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A project uses the Opus 5.5 audio codec to surface a previously unknown eyewitness record of the dodo, illustrating how archival audio tooling can reveal new historical evidence. The approach demonstrates how encoding choices can influence what evidence survives research. It also highlights the broader potential of modern codecs in digital archaeology and forensics.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个项目利用 Opus 5.5 音频编解码器揭示了关于渡渡鸟的一个新目击记录。该成果表明档案音频工具和编码选择会影响可用证据的保存与发现。也凸显了现代编解码技术在数字考古与取证中的潜在作用。</p>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(139) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.thenationalnews.com/future/technology/2026/09/29/ai-industry-needs-to-earn-6-trillion-by-2031-to-justify-data-centres/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI needs $6T in annual revenue to justify data centre boom</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI行业需要6万亿美元年度营收以支撑数据中心热潮</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49898952' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 221</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49898952' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 330</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AI-driven workloads are expected to justify a six-trillion-dollar annual revenue by 2031 to sustain data-center growth. The story explains how scaling AI demand, efficiency gains, and new business models must offset energy and infrastructure costs, or the hyperscale boom may stall.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>AI相关的数据中心需求正在以2031年前实现每年约6万亿美元的营收来支撑扩张。文章指出，AI工作负载、效率提升和新商业模式若不能抵消能源、降温和算力成本，数据中心扩张可能放缓。</p>

</div>

[pdf]</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>ADHD、自闭症还是复杂创伤？[PDF]</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49946403' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 221</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49946403' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 260</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A Cambridge Core paper argues that diagnosing ADHD, autism spectrum conditions, and complex trauma is not straightforward due to overlapping symptoms. It urges nuanced assessments and integration of developmental context and co-morbidities.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一篇剑桥核心论文指出，ADHD、自闭症谱系障碍和复杂创伤的区分并非易事，症状常有重叠，需要更细致的评估并综合发展背景与共病情况。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(145) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://nltimes.nl/2026/09/30/data-centers-refusing-say-much-water-electricity-use' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Most data centers refusing to say how much water, electricity they use</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>多数数据中心拒绝披露用水与用电量</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49907057' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 213</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49907057' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 197</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece reports that most data centers refuse to disclose water and electricity usage, complicating environmental accountability. Heightened scrutiny from regulators and investors is pushing for standardized metrics and transparent reporting. The lack of disclosure could affect sustainability initiatives and public trust.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>报道指出多数数据中心拒绝披露用水和用电量，增加了环境问责的复杂性。监管机构和投资者对标准化指标与透明披露的关注在上升，缺乏披露可能影响可持续发展计划和公众信任。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(146) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.breakthroughjournal.org/p/things-that-apparently-cause-cancer' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Things that apparently cause cancer</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>据称致癌的事物</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49940219' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 211</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49940219' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 77</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Health'>Health</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The article surveys items that are popularly claimed to cause cancer, emphasizing the difference between strong scientific consensus and sensational claims; it urges readers to rely on evidence-based risk assessments and avoid misinformation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章盘点被广泛声称会致癌的事物，强调科学共识与耸人听闻之间的区分；提醒读者以循证风险评估为准，避免错误信息。</p>
