<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49937540' data-tags='Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(147) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://matduggan.com/what-does-my-dream-os-ui-look-like/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Make Tmux the OS</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>让 Tmux 成为操作系统的核心界面</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49937540' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 211</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49937540' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 127</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This piece envisions an OS-like UX built around tmux, using the terminal multiplexer as the primary windowing system. It emphasizes keyboard-driven workflows, portability, and minimalism, while acknowledging UX and compatibility tradeoffs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文设想以 tmux 为核心，打造类操作系统的用户界面，把终端多路复用器作为主要的窗口系统，强调键盘驱动的工作流、可移植性与极简主义，同时也提及 UX 与兼容性的权衡。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://matduggan.com/content/images/2026/09/image-9.png' data-full='https://matduggan.com/content/images/2026/09/image-9.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49893653' data-tags='Business,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(148) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theregister.com/os-platforms/2026/09/29/google-ending-chromeos-support-two-years-early/5299674' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Google ending ChromeOS support two years early</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Google 提前两年结束 ChromeOS 支持</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49893653' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 210</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49893653' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 141</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Google is ending ChromeOS support for older devices two years earlier than planned, compressing the device retirement timeline. This tightens upgrade paths for schools and businesses while signaling a shift toward newer hardware and services.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Google将ChromeOS对部分老旧设备的支持提前两年结束，加速了退场。此举给依赖老旧硬件的学校和企业带来升级压力，也反映谷歌正将资源聚焦于新硬件与云端服务的战略调整。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://image.theregister.com/226413.jpg?imageId=226413&amp;x=0&amp;y=0&amp;cropw=100&amp;croph=100&amp;panox=0&amp;panoy=0&amp;panow=100&amp;panoh=100&amp;width=1200&amp;height=683' data-full='https://image.theregister.com/226413.jpg?imageId=226413&amp;x=0&amp;y=0&amp;cropw=100&amp;croph=100&amp;panox=0&amp;panoy=0&amp;panow=100&amp;panoh=100&amp;width=1200&amp;height=683' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49947051' data-tags='Programming,DevOps,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(149) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.cloudflare.com/next-git-platform-on-cloudflare/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>We want you to build the next Git platform on Cloudflare</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我们希望你在 Cloudflare 上打造下一个 Git 平台</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49947051' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 210</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49947051' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 190</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Cloudflare envisions enabling developers to build the next Git platform on its edge network, leveraging edge compute and storage. The plan addresses performance, security, and governance considerations for hosting a version-control stack at the edge.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Cloudflare 表示希望开发者能在其边缘网络上搭建下一个 Git 平台，利用边缘计算与存储实现协作。文章讨论在边缘托管版本控制栈时的性能、安全与治理等挑战。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blog.cloudflare.com/_emdash/api/media/file/01M3VGEQ8Q6FVNPPSWW71YRRKT.01M3VGER1EC7W9TEZXNR3GGMK7.png' data-full='https://blog.cloudflare.com/_emdash/api/media/file/01M3VGEQ8Q6FVNPPSWW71YRRKT.01M3VGER1EC7W9TEZXNR3GGMK7.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(150) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://techrights.org/n/2026/10/01/Red_Hat_Being_Phased_Out_of_Existence_Like_Many_Other_Companies.shtml' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Red Hat being phased out of existence?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Red Hat 将逐步边缘化？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49923056' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 210</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49923056' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 139</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The article questions whether Red Hat is being trimmed away as IBM consolidates cloud-native strategies. It points to signals like product overlap with IBM’s stack, leadership changes, and shifting investment priorities as evidence that the brand could lose its standalone prominence.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章质疑在 IBM 统一云原生策略之下，Red Hat 是否正逐步被削弱。指出与 IBM 云端栈的重叠、领导层调整和投资重点的变化等信号，可能让 Red Hat 的独立品牌地位逐渐淡出。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://techrights.org/images/header-pillars.jpg' data-full='https://techrights.org/images/header-pillars.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(151) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.home-assistant.io/blog/2026/10/02/big-tech-ruined-the-cloud-so-were-renaming-ours/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Big Tech ruined the cloud, so we&#x27;re renaming ours</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>大科技毁了云，我们将重命名自己的云</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49934228' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 209</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49934228' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 106</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Home Assistant explains a renaming of its cloud service to distance from big‑tech cloud baggage and emphasize privacy‑first, local‑control design. The move underscores how smaller platforms push back against dominant cloud narratives and seek clearer branding around user ownership.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Home Assistant 表示将其云服务更名，以摆脱大科技公司云服务的负担，强调隐私优先和本地控制的设计。这一改名反映了小型平台对主导云叙事的抵抗，以及寻求围绕用户掌控权的更清晰品牌定位。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.home-assistant.io/images/blog/2026-10-big-tech-ruined-the-cloud-so-were-renaming-ours/art.webp' data-full='https://www.home-assistant.io/images/blog/2026-10-big-tech-ruined-the-cloud-so-were-renaming-ours/art.webp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(152) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.cnbc.com/2026/09/30/ftc-ai-probe-openai-anthropic.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>FTC is investigating OpenAI, Anthropic and other AI companies over product risks</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>FTC 正在就 OpenAI、Anthropic 等 AI 公司的产品风险展开调查</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49921050' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 206</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49921050' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 158</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The FTC is probing potential product risks across major AI providers, including OpenAI and Anthropic, signaling intensified regulatory scrutiny over safety and consumer protection in AI offerings. The inquiry could raise compliance costs and influence product strategies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>FTC 正在就 OpenAI、Anthropic 等 AI 公司的产品风险展开调查，表明监管机构对 AI 产品的安全与消费者保护监管正在加强，可能增加合规成本并影响产品策略。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(153) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.1011now.com/2026/09/30/more-questions-than-answers-about-lincolns-google-data-center-water-electricity-usage/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Improper redaction reveals Google Data Center water and electricity usage</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>不当涂改暴露谷歌数据中心的水电消耗</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49957068' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 205</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49957068' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 302</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An improper redaction leaked detailed water and electricity usage for a Google data center, sparking questions about energy transparency in large-scale infrastructure. The disclosure underscores how energy footprints of hyperscale operators are increasingly scrutinized by regulators and the public.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>不当涂改导致某谷歌数据中心的用水与用电数据被披露，引发对大型基础设施能源透明度的关注。此类信息暴露凸显了对超大规模运营商能源足迹日益严格的监管与公众审视。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(154) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theguardian.com/environment/2026/sep/29/night-sky-darkness-city-regulation' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>We’re forgetting what darkness feels like</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>我们正在忘记夜晚的黑暗</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49898050' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 204</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49898050' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 122</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that modern life dims our experience of true darkness due to pervasive lighting, urging regulation or urban design changes to restore darker nights. It touches safety, ecology, and the tension between city life and starry skies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章认为由于普遍的照明，现代生活让人错失真正的黑暗体验，呼吁通过法规或城市设计来恢复更黑的夜色，涉及安全与生态等议题。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/0d7db760638867c2e64a657cd7408dcd02a8af1f/519_0_5184_4148/master/5184.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=3272627be719a709b035ddc693193521' data-full='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/0d7db760638867c2e64a657cd7408dcd02a8af1f/519_0_5184_4148/master/5184.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=3272627be719a709b035ddc693193521' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49879401' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 204</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49879401' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 96</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>HN.watch aggregates video representations of Hacker News posts, offering a new channel to consume the front page. It reflects a broader trend of turning text content into video formats.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>HN.watch 将 Hacker News 帖子以视频形式汇总，提供观看前页的新渠道，体现了把文本内容转为视频的趋势。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49874728' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(156) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://platform.claude.com/docs/en/build-with-claude/prompt-engineering/prompting-claude-opus-5-5' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Prompting Claude Opus 5.5</a></p>

<p class='hn-title'>(157) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://shnatsel.github.io/state-of-simd-rust-2026/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The state of SIMD in Rust in 2026</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Rust 在 2026 年的 SIMD 状态</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49844629' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 204</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49844629' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 60</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Rust&#x27;s SIMD story in 2026 shows the portable SIMD API maturing, with broader compiler support and ecosystem tools. Developers gain clearer performance paths for vectorized code across CPUs, but stabilizing features and crate parity remains a challenge.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>到 2026 年，Rust 的 SIMD 生态逐步成熟，便携式 SIMD API 得到更广泛的编译器支持和工具链。面向向量化的性能路径变得更清晰，但稳定特性与生态配套仍有挑战。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(158) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://theintercept.com/2026/09/24/how-many-flock-devices-in-united-states-300000/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Flock Wants the Most Detailed Map of Its Surveillance Cameras Taken Offline</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Flock 希望将其监控摄像头最详细的地图下线</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49884363' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 202</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49884363' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 101</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Flock is seeking to take offline the most detailed public map of its surveillance cameras, citing privacy and safety concerns. The move highlights the tension between transparency and security, and raises questions about how much detail about surveillance networks should be publicly accessible.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Flock 请求将其监控摄像头最详细的公开地图下线，理由是隐私与安全风险上升。这一举动凸显透明度与安全之间的权衡，并引发了关于监控网络细节公开程度的讨论。</p>

</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49941091' data-tags='Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(159) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.cloudflare.com/announcing-cloudflare-ohttp-gateway/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Cloudflare OHTTP gateway</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Cloudflare OHTTP 网关</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49941091' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 198</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49941091' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 95</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Cloudflare launches an OHTTP gateway that enables oblivious HTTP requests, reducing metadata leakage and improving user privacy when fetching resources. The feature fits into a broader trend toward privacy-preserving web protocols and could alter how clients interact with CDNs and origins.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Cloudflare 推出 OHTTP 网关，支持盲化的 HTTP 请求，降低元数据外泄风险并提升用户隐私。此举体现了向隐私保护网络协议的趋势，可能改变客户端与 CDN/源站之间的交互方式。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blog.cloudflare.com/_emdash/api/media/file/01M3XZ8QDSM1TSSYPVFW13B39E.01M3XZ8RDWEX6N8XXP6ZRDY0DK.png' data-full='https://blog.cloudflare.com/_emdash/api/media/file/01M3XZ8QDSM1TSSYPVFW13B39E.01M3XZ8RDWEX6N8XXP6ZRDY0DK.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49924618' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 198</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49924618' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 265</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Readers are invited to vote on which of Hacker News&#x27; listed AI challenges have been met. The poll distills community sentiment and tracks progress against ambitious benchmarks. Results could guide researchers and product teams prioritizing future work.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>社区成员被邀请对 Hacker News 列出的 AI 挑战已实现情况进行投票。投票结果反映社区进展情绪并对比设定的目标基准。结果可能为研究者和产品团队在未来工作重点上提供指引。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(161) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/magnitudedev/magnitude' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Launch HN: Magnitude (YC S25) – Self-optimizing inference engine for agents</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Launch HN: Magnitude (YC S25) – 面向代理的自我优化推理引擎</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49911995' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 194</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49911995' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 97</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Magnitude is a self-optimizing inference engine designed for agents, automatically tuning runtimes and workloads to boost performance and efficiency. It sits at the intersection of AI agent frameworks and efficient inference, promising to reduce manual tuning and hardware waste. Backed by YC S25, it signals growing interest in tooling for autonomous agents.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Magnitude 是一个面向代理的自我优化推理引擎，能够自动调参以提升推理性能和能效，减少人工调优工作。它处在 AI 代理框架与高效推理技术的交汇处，代表了为自走代理提升算力利用率的一条新路径。由 YC S25 支持，反映了对自动化代理工具的热切关注。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49944912' data-tags='DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(165) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ftl-os.org/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>FTL: A new operating system for clouds</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>FTL：云端新型操作系统</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49944912' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 192</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49944912' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 74</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>FTL envisions a cloud-native operating system that unifies compute, storage, and services across distributed environments. It emphasizes a lean, event-driven control plane and aims to reduce vendor lock-in while improving performance guarantees for multi-cloud workloads.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>FTL 构想为云原生操作系统，试图在分布式环境中统一计算、存储与服务。它强调以事件驱动的控制平面，并致力于降低厂商锁定、提升多云工作负载的性能与可观测性。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49891203' data-tags='AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(167) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://magazine.sebastianraschka.com/p/classifier-history-and-jev' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Language models for text classification: From bag-of-words to Jev</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>文本分类的语言模型：从词袋到 JEV</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49891203' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 189</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49891203' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 10</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Offers a retrospective on text classification models, tracing the evolution from bag-of-words to modern embedding-based approaches and introducing JEV as a contemporary technique. The overview helps readers understand when simple representations suffice and when richer models are warranted.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文回顾文本分类模型的发展，从词袋模型到现代的嵌入式方法，并介绍了 JEV 作为当代技术的一个示例。该综述帮助读者理解在何种场景下简单表示即可，何时需要更丰富的模型。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(171) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://twitter.com/GayaniFigma/status/2105295629941350454' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Figma restricts MCP access to whitelisted clients, excluding Pi</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Figma 将 MCP 访问限制为白名单客户端，排除树莓派</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49922729' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 187</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49922729' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 106</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Figma has restricted MCP access to a whitelist of clients, effectively excluding Raspberry Pi devices. The change highlights how access controls can fragment an ecosystem and impact hobbyists and budget setups that rely on inexpensive hardware.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Figma 将 MCP 访问限定为白名单客户端，树莓派等设备因此被排除在外。这一变动揭示了访问控制如何分裂生态系统，并影响依赖低成本硬件的爱好者和小型部署。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(172) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://news.synopsys.com/2026-09-30-OpenAI-and-Synopsys-Announce-GPT-Synopsys-Frontier-Intelligence-to-Revolutionize-Chip-Design' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>GPT-Synopsys: Frontier Intelligence to Revolutionize Chip Design</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GPT-Synopsys：Frontier Intelligence 将革新芯片设计</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49919910' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 186</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49919910' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 110</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Synopsys and OpenAI announced GPT-Synopsys Frontier Intelligence, a collaboration to bring large-language-model-driven automation into chip design. The goal is to speed up architectural exploration, design verification, and documentation, potentially shortening production cycles, but it also raises questions about reliability, data governance, and IP security in critical engineering work.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Synopsys 与 OpenAI 公布 GPT-Synopsys Frontier Intelligence，旨在把大语言模型驱动的自动化引入芯片设计流程。目标包括加速架构探索、验证和文档编制，可能缩短上市周期，但也带来对可靠性、数据治理和知识产权安全的担忧。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A geometric puzzle about shaping a tank body under simple constraints, yielding surprising optimal configurations. The problem illustrates how intuition can mislead in constrained geometry and demonstrates elegant reasoning tricks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个关于在简单约束下设计坦克车身的几何难题，往往产生出人意料的最优解。该问题展示了在受限几何中直觉往往会误导，并揭示了优雅的推理技巧。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(175) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.quora.com/Did-the-ENIAC-have-a-BIOS/answer/Alan-Kay-11' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Alan Kay&#x27;s answer to “Did the ENIAC have a BIOS”?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>艾伦·凯对“ENIAC是否有 BIOS”的回答</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49870070' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 185</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49870070' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 74</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Alan Kay clarifies that ENIAC did not have a BIOS by modern definitions; it used hardware wiring and manual bootstrapping rather than firmware. The note sheds light on how early machines booted and how software-era concepts evolved.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>艾伦·凯澄清，ENIAC 不具备现代 BIOS 的概念，它依靠硬件布线和手动引导，而非固件。此说法帮助理解早期计算机的启动机制，以及固件如何演化成我们熟悉的接口。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(177) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://oilprice.com/Latest-Energy-News/World-News/US-Strategic-Petroleum-Reserve-Falls-to-Lowest-Level-Since-1982.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>U.S. Strategic Petroleum Reserve Falls to Lowest Level Since 1982</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美国战略石油储备降至1982年以来的最低水平</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49887337' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 180</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49887337' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 229</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The U.S. Strategic Petroleum Reserve has fallen to its lowest level since 1982 due to ongoing releases intended to stabilize fuel prices. The depletion underscores the trade-off between short-term price stability and long-term energy security, with potential implications for future policy and market resilience.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>美国战略石油储备因持续释放以稳定燃油价格，降至1982年以来的最低水平。这折射出短期价格稳定与长期能源安全之间的权衡，并可能影响未来的政策走向与市场韧性。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(179) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.habibicode.org/thedrawnworld' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>37,500 border drawings: a map of the world as people remember it</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>37,500 条边界绘画：人们记忆中的世界地图</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49875142' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 175</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49875142' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 32</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A crowdsourced map of 37,500 border drawings reveals how people remember geopolitical boundaries—often diverging from official maps. The project spotlights memory, bias, and the human dimension of mapmaking, with implications for education and diplomacy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个汇集了 37,500 条边界绘画的项目，揭示了人们记忆中的地缘边界往往与官方地图存在差异。该作品聚焦记忆、偏见以及地图绘制的人文维度，对教育和外交有一定影响。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49873241' data-tags='AI,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(180) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2110.01834' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Thinking fast and slow in AI: The role of metacognition (2021)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI中的快思考与慢思考：元认知的作用（2021）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49873241' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 175</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49873241' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 77</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>AI systems can benefit from metacognitive capabilities—reflecting on their own reasoning, critiquing steps, and calibrating confidence. This line of research borrows cognitive science to improve reliability, safety, and interpretability in AI, helping models avoid overconfident errors.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>AI 系统若具备元认知能力，能够反思自身推理、纠错并校准信心。这类研究将认知科学引入机器学习，提升 AI 的可靠性、可解释性和安全性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' data-full='https://arxiv.org/static/browse/0.3.4/images/arxiv-logo-fb.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(182) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://alphapixeldev.com/sdf-vs-msdf-vs-slug-vs-rive-gpu-text-rendering/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>SDF vs. MSDF vs. Slug: GPU Text Rendering</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>GPU 字体渲染：SDF、MSDF、Slug 对比</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49908962' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 171</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49908962' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 68</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post compares SDF, MSDF, Slug, and Rive approaches for GPU-based text rendering, highlighting how multi-channel signed distance fields reduce blurriness and improve edge sharpness across fonts. It discusses tradeoffs in atlas size, preprocessing, and runtime performance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章对比了 SDF、MSDF、Slug 与 Rive 在 GPU 字体渲染中的差异，指出多通道 Signed Distance Fields 如何提升字体边缘锐度与一致性。并讨论了图集大小、预处理和运行时性能等权衡。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://alphapixeldev.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/slughorn-vs-sdf-msdf-rive-grazing-perspective.png' data-full='https://alphapixeldev.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/slughorn-vs-sdf-msdf-rive-grazing-perspective.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(183) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://status.claude.com/incidents/4xvtc2gnq73l' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Claude partial outage</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Claude 部分性中断</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49893876' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 171</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49893876' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 146</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Claude experienced a partial outage that affected a subset of users or features. The incident underscores how even major AI services can face reliability challenges, highlighting the importance of robust incident response and communication for enterprise users.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Claude 遭遇部分性中断，影响到部分用户或功能。事件凸显即使是大型人工智能服务也会遇到可靠性挑战，强调对企业用户在故障响应与沟通方面的必要性。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(184) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37725' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Context Language Models</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>上下文语言模型</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49922437' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 168</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49922437' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 46</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Context Language Models paper argues for treating context as a separate, scalable dimension in LLMs, enabling dynamic memory, retrieval-augmented reasoning, and better long-horizon tasks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>论文提出将上下文作为可扩展维度来管理，使语言模型能够通过外部记忆与检索增强推理，改善长时间依赖任务的表现。</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>该指南展示了如何利用 Conan 与 CMake 将任意 C++ 库集成到 Godot 引擎中。方法让开发者能够在 Godot 中使用原生代码、提升性能并复用现有的 C++ 生态。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(188) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://somethingbig.ai/computer' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>NAND-16: a computer built from 277,248 NAND gates</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>NAND-16：由277,248个NAND门构成的计算机</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49871018' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 165</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49871018' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 98</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>NAND-16 demonstrates a working computer built entirely from NAND gates, totaling 277,248 gates. The project showcases how complex systems can be constructed from simple primitives and offers educational insight into computer architecture.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>NAND-16 用 277,248 个 NAND 门构成一台可运行的计算机，展示了从最基本逻辑单元构建复杂系统的思路，提供对计算机体系结构的教育性洞见。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(189) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://bookofrevenue.com/blog/6ab81e9a97a13f0001f7e4e1/postgres-at-time-zone-u-does-not-do-what-you-think-it-does' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Footguns with Postgres “at time zone &#x27;UTC&#x27;”</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Postgres 的“AT TIME ZONE &#x27;UTC&#x27;”踩坑</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49865312' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 165</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49865312' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 103</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The post warns that AT TIME ZONE &#x27;UTC&#x27; in Postgres does not behave as many expect, leading to subtle timestamp errors. It clarifies the actual behavior and offers pragmatic guidance to avoid common pitfalls in data pipelines.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文中指出 Postgres 的 AT TIME ZONE &#x27;UTC&#x27; 的行为常被误解，容易在时间戳转换中引发细微的错误。给出实际行为解释和实用建议，帮助避免数据管道中的常见坑。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://bookofrevenue.com/assets/images/bb5d5899f499a7ad369d587be86672c2-blog-og-image.jpg' data-full='https://bookofrevenue.com/assets/images/bb5d5899f499a7ad369d587be86672c2-blog-og-image.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(190) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://archaeologymag.com/2026/09/gobeklitepe-burials-hundreds-of-scattered-bones/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>12,000-year-old Göbeklitepe burials explain scattered bones</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>12000年前的戈贝克利泰佩墓葬揭示骨骼散落原因</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49855059' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 165</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49855059' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 49</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>New analysis of Göbeklitepe bones suggests why some skeletons were scattered across the site, offering fresh clues about burial practices and ritual use at one of humanity&#x27;s oldest temples.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对戈贝克利泰佩约1.2万年前骨骼的新分析，揭示骨骼散落的原因，为这个古老神庙的安葬习俗与仪式用途提供新线索。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49938616' data-tags='Business,Startups,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(191) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.sshh.io/p/the-harness-is-the-company' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Every SaaS business will become a harness around a model</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>每个 SaaS 企业都会成为围绕模型运转的载体</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49938616' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 164</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49938616' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 108</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that SaaS businesses are increasingly built as interfaces to a central AI model, with the model becoming the core asset. Companies will monetize by providing access, latency, data governance, and model-management layers rather than traditional feature bundles.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章认为 SaaS 业务正逐步演变为对接一个核心 AI 模型的入口，模型成为关键资产。未来的商业模式将围绕模型访问、延迟、数据治理与模型管理等层次，而非单纯的功能包。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!DMig!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Fd8cf3fdf-c311-47d0-b42d-f75864c4dd68_1620x897.png' data-full='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!DMig!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Fd8cf3fdf-c311-47d0-b42d-f75864c4dd68_1620x897.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>C++ Insights exposes how your code looks to the compiler, revealing template instantiation and overload resolution in action. This helps developers understand surprising behavior and write more robust, well-abstracted code.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>C++ Insights 将你的代码呈现为编译器的视角，揭示模板实例化与重载解析等后台过程。它帮助开发者理解意料之外的行为，并编写更健壮、抽象层次更清晰的代码。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(200) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://ihavenapkinthoughts.substack.com/p/burning-man-death-rates-a-short-lesson' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Burning Man death rates – A short lesson in statistics</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Burning Man 死亡率：一个统计学教训</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49882754' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 158</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49882754' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 201</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece uses Burning Man death rates to illustrate basic statistics pitfalls like small-sample variability and selection bias. It emphasizes how sensational statistics can mislead without proper context, and demonstrates how to think about rates per population at events.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文用 Burning Man 的死亡率来揭示小样本变异性与选择偏差等统计陷阱。若缺乏恰当背景，统计数字容易被误解；文中展示了如何用事件规模来合理解读死亡率等比率。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(202) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/dashersw/coyopedal' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Guitar amp and effects pedal built on the Waveshare ESP32-S3-Touch-AMOLED-2.06</a></p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A hands-on project builds a guitar amp and effects pedal around the ESP32-S3 with a touch display, showcasing how affordable microcontrollers can power portable, customizable audio gear. It demonstrates hardware hacking, real-time audio processing, and DIY creativity.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这是一个用 ESP32-S3 和触控显示屏打造吉他放大器与效果器的动手项目，展示了低成本微控制器如何实现便携、可定制的音频设备。项目融合硬件黑客、实时音频处理与创意实验。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/5da6c628f96b61c03ed11e97a15f38de8b8581215d5fde8429dada93107f17c6/dashersw/coyopedal' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/5da6c628f96b61c03ed11e97a15f38de8b8581215d5fde8429dada93107f17c6/dashersw/coyopedal' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49954882' data-tags='Privacy,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(203) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://automatictransmission.khoury.northeastern.edu/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Car is a smartphone on wheels. Here&#x27;s who&#x27;s listening</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>车载数据像行驶中的智能手机：谁在监听</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49954882' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 157</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49954882' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 77</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Connected cars leak data about driver behavior, location, and voice commands to a mix of manufacturers and third parties. The piece maps who receives this data and why, highlighting privacy implications and ways consumers can tighten controls.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>连网汽车会将驾驶行为、位置信息和语音指令等数据传送给厂商及第三方。内容梳理了谁会接收这些数据以及背后的原因，揭示隐私风险并给出控制数据共享的思路。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://automatictransmission.khoury.northeastern.edu/images/test-facility.png' data-full='https://automatictransmission.khoury.northeastern.edu/images/test-facility.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49904408' data-tags='Finance'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(204) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://electrek.co/2026/09/29/tesla-takes-on-30-billion-in-credit-as-it-approaches-unprofitability/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Tesla takes on $30B in credit as it approaches unprofitability</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>特斯拉在盈利困境边缘时获批300亿美元信贷</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49904408' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 156</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49904408' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 217</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Tesla arranges about $30 billion in credit facilities as it approaches profitability challenges, illustrating ongoing financing needs amid aggressive capital expenditure for scaling. The move underscores how automakers rely on debt markets during high-capital phases.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>特斯拉在盈利能力趋紧之际获得约300亿美元的信贷，凸显在扩张与高资本开支阶段对融资的持续需求。这反映出汽车厂商在高资本阶段对债务市场的依赖。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://electrek.co/wp-content/uploads/sites/3/2025/05/Elon-Musk-Tesla-Money-hero.png?resize=1200,628' data-full='https://electrek.co/wp-content/uploads/sites/3/2025/05/Elon-Musk-Tesla-Money-hero.png?resize=1200,628' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49945323' data-tags='Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(205) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.radical-elements.com/minor-epiphanies/city-building-games-have-a-soul-problem-pt2' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>City building games have a Soul Problem pt.2</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>城市建造游戏的灵魂问题（下篇）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49945323' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 156</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49945323' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 147</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>City-building games often struggle to convey a meaningful sense of soul, character, and player agency. The piece argues designers should balance emergent systems with thematic cohesion, using constraints and storytelling to give cities personality.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>城市建造游戏常常难以传达真正的灵魂与角色感以及玩家的掌控感。文章主张在系统深度与题材连贯性之间取得平衡，通过约束与叙事赋予城市独特个性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.radical-elements.com/storage/files/cities-article-cover-6ac0e0f77928c.png' data-full='https://www.radical-elements.com/storage/files/cities-article-cover-6ac0e0f77928c.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(206) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://con.racket-lang.org/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>RacketCon Is Saturday</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>RacketCon 将在周六举行</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49922515' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 156</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49922515' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 49</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>RacketCon is happening this Saturday, spotlighting the Racket language community, tooling, and education. Speakers will cover language design, macro systems, and practical projects, underscoring the vitality of Lisp-family ecosystems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>RacketCon 将于周六举行，聚焦 Racket 语言社区、工具链与教育。演讲主题涵盖语言设计、宏系统与实际项目，显示 Lisp 家族生态的活力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://con.racket-lang.org/rcon2026logo.png' data-full='https://con.racket-lang.org/rcon2026logo.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49933461' data-tags='Data,DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(207) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://mapped.earth/lighthouses/world' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A map of every lighthouse</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>全球灯塔一览地图</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49933461' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 156</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49933461' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 72</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The global lighthouse map aggregates location data to visualize every lighthouse, revealing patterns in maritime infrastructure and historical shipping routes. The project blends geospatial data with storytelling about navigation safety and coastal development, offering insights for researchers and enthusiasts.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这份全球灯塔地图汇集位置数据，直观呈现全球灯塔分布及海上航运网络的演变。通过可视化地理信息，展示了航海安全与沿海开发的历史与现状，适合研究者与海事爱好者。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://mapped.earth/api/og/lighthouses' data-full='https://mapped.earth/api/og/lighthouses' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-title'>(208) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://frostyard.github.io/nsl/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: NSL – WSL for Linux</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：NSL——Linux 的 WSL</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49894351' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 153</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49894351' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 97</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Introducing NSL, a WSL-style subsystem that brings Linux user-space directly into a Linux host, aiming for seamless cross-environment development.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>介绍 NSL，这个类似 WSL 的子系统将 Linux 用户空间直接带入 Linux 主机，目标是实现无缝的跨环境开发。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://frostyard.github.io/nsl/assets/nsl.svg' data-full='https://frostyard.github.io/nsl/assets/nsl.svg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(209) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://statmodeling.stat.columbia.edu/2026/08/27/258/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Nicholas Polson has authored 258 academic papers in 2026 so far</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>尼古拉斯·波尔森在2026年迄今已发表258篇学术论文</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49898877' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 151</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49898877' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 110</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Polson has already authored 258 papers in 2026, a blistering output that illustrates intense productivity in statistics and data science.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>截至2026年8月，尼古拉斯·波尔森已发表258篇学术论文，展现出极高的产出速度。文章讨论了这一节奏对学术文化、合作方式以及研究传播速度的影响。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(210) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.africanistperspective.com/p/on-the-escalation-of-war-in-ethiopia' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Escalation of War in Ethiopia</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>埃塞俄比亚战争升级</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49943451' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 151</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49943451' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 160</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The conflict in Ethiopia has escalated, with fighting spreading and humanitarian crises intensifying. The piece analyzes political dynamics, regional implications, and the impact on aid delivery and digital information ecosystems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>埃塞俄比亚冲突升级，战事扩散与人道危机加剧。文章分析了政治动态、地区影响以及对援助投送和数字信息生态的冲击。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!O1tC!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Fea7d3580-b56e-458e-bee7-b2f9c6446172_1280x853.jpeg' data-full='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!O1tC!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Fea7d3580-b56e-458e-bee7-b2f9c6446172_1280x853.jpeg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49884625' data-tags='Hardware,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(211) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/Low-Zi-Hong/ESP32s3-LLM-Cluster' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>ESP32S3 cluster running 1.58-bit (BitNet) Language model</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>ESP32S3 集群运行 1.58 位量化的 BitNet 语言模型</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49884625' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 150</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49884625' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 31</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An ESP32-S3 cluster runs a 1.58-bit BitNet language model, pushing edge AI further into tiny hardware. The demo showcases continued progress in running language models on embedded devices, raising questions about practicality, latency, and use cases.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>ESP32S3 集群在边缘硬件上运行一个 1.58 位量化的 BitNet 语言模型，推动边缘AI走向更小的硬件。该演示展示在嵌入式设备上运行语言模型的持续进展，同时引发关于可用性、延迟和小型模型训练的讨论。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/09e3f3f9512f854ec7f391f1287bc1c2d9528a83365360add29b2bc255080ab5/Low-Zi-Hong/ESP32s3-LLM-Cluster' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/09e3f3f9512f854ec7f391f1287bc1c2d9528a83365360add29b2bc255080ab5/Low-Zi-Hong/ESP32s3-LLM-Cluster' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49869755' data-tags='Design,Privacy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(212) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.inkandswitch.com/essay/malleable-software/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Malleable software: Restoring user agency in a world of locked-down apps (2025)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>可塑性软件：在被封锁的应用世界中重塑用户自主权（2025）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49869755' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 150</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49869755' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 71</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Contends for malleable software that users can inspect, modify, and repair, even as apps lock down features. Proposes design norms and platform incentives to reclaim user agency, privacy, and resilience against vendor-driven lock-in.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>主张让软件具备可塑性，用户可以查看、修改和修复，即使应用方锁定功能。提出设计原则和平台激励，以重获用户自主权、隐私和对抗供应商锁定的韧性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://inkandswitch.com/essay/malleable-software/guitar.jpg' data-full='https://inkandswitch.com/essay/malleable-software/guitar.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49937916' data-tags='Show HN,AI,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(213) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/anteloc/ldraw-nova' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Made an open-source Lego AI generator</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：开源乐高 AI 生成器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49937916' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 150</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49937916' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 49</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A community-driven open-source tool creates LEGO-ready designs using AI-assisted generation and LDraw data. It demonstrates how AI can empower hobbyists to prototype and fabricate physical models.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一个社区驱动的开源工具，使用 AI 辅助生成和 LDraw 数据来创建可用于乐高拼搭的设计，展示了 AI 如何帮助爱好者快速原型并制作实体模型。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/fb1590186101c74ab726144431a58a6a612a81fe2ff7fed82cad6a29cf8a51e6/anteloc/ldraw-nova' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/fb1590186101c74ab726144431a58a6a612a81fe2ff7fed82cad6a29cf8a51e6/anteloc/ldraw-nova' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49951881' data-tags='Energy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(214) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://energytransition.org/2026/09/in-ukraine-distributed-renewables-foil-russias-assaults/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>In Ukraine, distributed renewables foil Russia&#x27;s assaults</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>在乌克兰，分散式可再生能源抵御俄罗斯袭击</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49951881' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 150</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49951881' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 160</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Distributed renewables in Ukraine are increasing energy resilience by decentralizing power, reducing exposure to centralized grid attacks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>乌克兰的分散式可再生能源提高了能源韧性，通过去中心化供电降低对集中电网的脆弱性。此举在冲突中提升了民用和军事层面的抗压能力。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://energytransition.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/caspar-rae-vu3pOspbvN0-unsplash-scaled.jpg' data-full='https://energytransition.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/caspar-rae-vu3pOspbvN0-unsplash-scaled.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49857281' data-tags='Programming,Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(215) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.adaptivecapacitylabs.com/2026/08/24/there-is-more-to-code-review-than-automatable-detection/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>There is more to code review than (automatable) detection</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>代码评审不仅仅是可自动化检测</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49857281' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 149</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49857281' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 97</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Code reviews hinge on human context and design reasoning, not just automated checks. They help surface intent, improve maintainability, and spread knowledge across teams.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>代码评审不仅仅关乎自动化检测，它依赖于人际反馈、设计意图和共同学习。评审有助于揭示设计初衷、提升可维护性，并推动知识在团队之间传播。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.adaptivecapacitylabs.com/wp-content/uploads/2023/07/ACLsiteLogoWhite.png' data-full='https://www.adaptivecapacitylabs.com/wp-content/uploads/2023/07/ACLsiteLogoWhite.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49888182' data-tags='Business,Finance,Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(216) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://theconversation.com/when-oil-prices-spike-where-does-the-money-go-280763' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>When oil prices spike, where does the money go?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>油价暴涨时，利润去了哪里？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49888182' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 145</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49888182' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 148</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Explains how oil price spikes propagate through the supply chain, showing who benefits and who bears the cost. Highlights producer margins, refining profits, and transport contracts, and why pump prices don&#x27;t always track crude moves.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>解释了油价上升如何在供应链中重新分配利润，谁获利、谁承压。重点讨论生产商利润、炼厂收益与运输合同，以及为何加油站价格不总是与原油价格同步。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://images.theconversation.com/files/730536/original/file-20260416-63-94sb16.jpg?ixlib=rb-4.1.1&amp;rect=0%2C484%2C5799%2C2899&amp;q=45&amp;auto=format&amp;w=1356&amp;h=668&amp;fit=crop' data-full='https://images.theconversation.com/files/730536/original/file-20260416-63-94sb16.jpg?ixlib=rb-4.1.1&amp;rect=0%2C484%2C5799%2C2899&amp;q=45&amp;auto=format&amp;w=1356&amp;h=668&amp;fit=crop' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49876052' data-tags='DevOps'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(217) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.christianperone.com/2026/09/the-systems-that-no-one-will-test/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Systems that no one will test</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>无人测试的系统</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49876052' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 145</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49876052' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 77</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A critical look at how software and systems can fail in the wild when no one tests them under real-world conditions. The piece argues for robust testing strategies, including end-to-end tests, chaos engineering, and realistic load scenarios, to avoid unseen outages.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>讨论在真实世界条件下未经过充分测试的系统可能带来的风险。强调需要全面的测试策略、端到端测试和现实场景下的故障注入，以避免隐性故障。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blog.christianperone.com/wp-content/uploads/2024/06/guitarone.jpg' data-full='https://blog.christianperone.com/wp-content/uploads/2024/06/guitarone.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(218) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://fakecloud.dev/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Fakecloud: Local AWS cloud emulator for integration tests</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Fakecloud：用于集成测试的本地 AWS 云模拟器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49856885' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 145</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49856885' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 73</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Fakecloud provides a local, AWS-compatible cloud environment ideal for running integration tests offline. It helps teams test AWS interactions without credentials or network access, speeding up CI.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Fakecloud 提供一个本地、与 AWS 兼容的云环境，适合离线执行集成测试，帮助团队在没有凭证或网络的情况下测试 AWS 相关交互。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>An urbex-style look at Woking&#x27;s electrical control room from 2016 reveals the intricate layout and aging infrastructure of a mid-century utility facility. <p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对沃金电力控制室（2016）的实地影像揭示了这座中世纪公用事业设施的复杂布局与老化基础设施。照片凸显了关键基础设施的隐匿复杂性，并引发对保存与维护的思考。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Without the Hot Air is a data driven resource that challenges common myths in energy and climate policy with rigorous analysis. The piece advocates for clear metrics, skepticism of sensational claims, and practical steps toward efficiency and decarbonization.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>无废话的能源科普以数据驱动的方式挑战常见的能源与气候政策误区，强调用清晰的指标进行评估，并推动提高能效和低碳化的务实路径。</p>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>宗教学者与 Anthropic 讨论人工智能在道德抉择和符合多元伦理传统方面的设计。对话凸显跨文化视角在安全规范与治理中的作用。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(222) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.wired.com/story/ice-has-been-dumping-protester-photos-into-a-palantir-database/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>ICE Has Been Dumping Protester Photos into a Palantir Database</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>ICE 将抗议者照片汇入 Palantir 数据库</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49938477' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 144</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49938477' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 41</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>It reveals that ICE has been uploading protester photos into a Palantir-backed database, raising privacy and civil-liberties concerns. The story highlights the risks of centralized surveillance and the role of data provenance in public-interest tech policy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>报道揭示 ICE 将抗议者照片汇入 Palantir 数据库，引发隐私和公民自由方面的担忧。事件凸显集权化监控的风险，以及数据来源可追溯性在公共利益科技政策中的作用。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>RS developer confirms work on a new RuneScape MMO, signaling the series&#x27; expansion into a fresh, modern platform. The project aims to leverage existing fanbase while introducing updated visuals and scalable online gameplay, potentially revisiting the game&#x27;s classic hybrid between nostalgic design and contemporary online mechanics.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>官方宣布将开发一款新的《RuneScape》大型多人在线游戏，标志着系列在新平台上的扩张。新作将结合熟悉的玩法与现代化画面与在线机制，目的是在保留老玩家情怀的同时引入更高的可扩展性和新鲜体验。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(225) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.arxiv.org/2026/10/01/updated-rate-limit-policy/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>ArXiv&#x27;s Updated Rate Limit Policy</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>ArXiv 的更新速率限制政策</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49926512' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 141</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49926512' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 70</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>ArXiv announces stricter rate limits for API and automated access to curb abuse, affecting researchers running mass downloads or data scrapers. The changes emphasize fair use and recommend registering API keys and respecting quotas to avoid blocks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>ArXiv 宣布对 API 与自动访问实施更严格的速率限制以防滥用，这将影响需要批量下载数据的研究人员。新规定强调公正使用，建议注册 API 密钥并遵守配额以避免被封禁。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://blog.arxiv.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/10/arXiv-Monthly-Submissions-September-2026.png' data-full='https://blog.arxiv.org/wp-content/uploads/2026/10/arXiv-Monthly-Submissions-September-2026.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-title'>(226) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.gevernova.com/news/press-releases/nrc-issues-first-us-construction-permit-bwrx-300-small-modular-reactor-tva-clinch-river' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>NRC issues first U.S. construction permit for a BWRX-300 small modular reactor</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>NRC 颁发美国首张 BWRX-300 小型模块化反应堆建设许可</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49902019' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 140</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49902019' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 168</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The NRC issued the first U.S. construction permit for a BWRX-300 small modular reactor, marking a milestone for SMR deployment. The project at TVA&#x27;s Clinch River site exemplifies renewed interest in modular nuclear energy and tests licensing pathways.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>美国核规制委员会颁发了首张 BWRX-300 小型模块化反应堆建设许可，标志着美国微型反应堆部署迈出关键一步。田纳西河 TVA 的 Clinch River 项目体现了对模块化核能的重新关注，并测试了许可流程。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A Windows quirk causes Explorer to crash when handling Video CD metadata or drivers; a reminder that legacy media formats still pose edge-case bugs. The post underscores the challenge of handling older media pipelines in modern OSes.</p>
