<p class='hn-title'>(236) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/kvoltmer/Audionaut' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Audionaut – an open-source cross-platform multitrack audio editor</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：Audionaut——一个开源跨平台的多轨音频编辑器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49931031' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 128</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49931031' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 44</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Audionaut is an open-source, cross-platform multitrack audio editor that aims to empower creators with a community-driven alternative to proprietary tools. It supports recording, editing, and mixing workflows across Windows, macOS, and Linux, inviting collaboration and rapid feature iteration. This project signals a broader move toward open-source, customizable audio tooling for indie producers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Audionaut 是一个开源、跨平台的多轨音频编辑器，旨在为创作者提供开源替代品，促进社区协作与快速迭代。它支持 Windows、macOS 和 Linux，覆盖录制、编辑、混音等工作流程，鼓励共同开发新功能。该项目代表了开源音频工具在独立创作者生态中的潜在增长。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/2f2a68b9ab3a63cf3eca91f92d465f8f5b128e0df4eac12e4a3b2a7f88cc9d0a/kvoltmer/Audionaut' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/2f2a68b9ab3a63cf3eca91f92d465f8f5b128e0df4eac12e4a3b2a7f88cc9d0a/kvoltmer/Audionaut' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：JBR-001——一个开源3D可打印桌面机器人</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49890707' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 126</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49890707' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 31</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>JBR-001 is an open-source, 3D-printable desktop robot designed for hands-on learning and rapid experimentation. Built around an Arduino Uno, the project emphasizes accessibility and low-cost robotics. It serves as a practical blueprint for hobbyists to prototype small autonomous or interactive assistants.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>JBR-001 是一个开源、可3D打印的桌面机器人，面向动手学习和快速试验。以 Arduino Uno 为核心，强调可及性与低成本机器人应用，为爱好者提供了原型化小型自主或交互助手的实用蓝本。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Emitting metadata early reduces repeated work during compilation, speeding up the development loop. This approach can halve build and check times, sharpening feedback for changes and guiding compiler design.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>提前输出元数据显著减少编译过程中的重复工作，加速增量构建并提升反馈速度。此举对构建系统和编译器设计也有启示。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(248) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nytimes.com/2026/09/26/business/dealbook/ai-law-discount-billable-hour.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>As A.I. makes law firms more efficient, clients ask: &#x27;Where&#x27;s my discount?&#x27;</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI提高律师事务所效率，客户问：我的折扣在哪里？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49872522' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 117</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49872522' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 122</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>As AI makes law firms more efficient, clients press for discounts on billable hours, reshaping pricing models in professional services. The trend raises questions about value, automation, and how lawyers price expertise.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>AI 提高律师事务所效率的同时，客户要求折扣和新的定价模式，重新塑造了专业服务的定价结构。讨论自动化对价值与定价的影响。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49883536' data-tags='Science,Space,Energy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(249) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://arstechnica.com/science/2026/09/scientists-solve-1840s-space-weather-mystery/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Scientists solve 1840s space weather mystery</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>科学家破解1840年代太空天气之谜</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49883536' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 115</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49883536' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 48</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers solve a century-old space weather puzzle by reanalyzing 1840s solar activity, refining models of solar storms and their impact on Earth. The finding improves understanding of historical aurora data and informs modern preparedness.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究人员通过重新分析1840年代的太阳活动，破解了一个百年未解的太空天气难题，改进了对太阳风暴及其对地球影响的模型。这一发现有助于重建历史极光记录并提升对现代空间天气的应对。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://cdn.arstechnica.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/spaceweather-1152x648.jpg' data-full='https://cdn.arstechnica.net/wp-content/uploads/2026/09/spaceweather-1152x648.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(251) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://this.os.isfine.org/blog/posts/what-reverse-engineering-and-modernising-an-old-war-game-tells-us-about-the-econ/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>What reversing, modernising old games tells us about the economic impact of AI</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>逆向工程与现代化老游戏揭示 AI 的经济影响</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49861755' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 113</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49861755' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 58</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece uses reverse engineering and modernization of an old war game to draw lessons about AI&#x27;s economic impact, including productivity and labor displacement patterns. It argues how maintenance vs new development costs shape AI adoption.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>通过对一款旧战争游戏的逆向工程与现代化，该文讨论 AI 对经济的影响，例如生产力提升与劳动替代的模式，并分析维护成本与新开发成本如何推动 AI 的采用。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(252) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.neowin.net/news/system76-bans-ai-generated-code-across-many-of-its-cosmic-codebases/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Pop!_OS bans AI-generated code from much of its codebase</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Pop!_OS 禁止大部分代码中使用 AI 生成的代码</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49946321' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 113</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49946321' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 161</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>System76&#x27;s Pop!_OS bans AI-generated code from much of its codebase to avoid licensing, provenance, and reliability concerns. The move signals growing caution in open-source projects about the reliability and attribution of AI-produced software.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>System76 的 Pop!_OS 禁止在大部分代码库中使用 AI 生成的代码，以避免许可、来源与可靠性方面的问题。这一举措反映了开源项目在 AI 生成代码的可靠性与归属问题上的日益谨慎。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Show HN introduces TinyAIArena, a platform where small AI agents compete in a sandboxed arena. It showcases how lightweight agents interact, evolve strategies, and what it reveals about agent design.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Show HN 展示了 TinyAIArena，一个让小型 AI 代理人在沙箱中对决的平台，展示代理设计的对比和演化。</p>

</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49925742' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(261) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://agupubs.onlinelibrary.wiley.com/doi/10.1029/2026AV002570' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Oxygen-deprived underwater zones may not be “dead zones” but clue to early life</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>氧气缺乏的水下区域可能不仅仅是“死区”，而是早期生命的线索</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49925742' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 107</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49925742' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 15</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>New research suggests oxygen minimum zones, long seen as lifeless, might hold clues about early life on Earth and how life adapted to low-oxygen environments. The findings could reshape our understanding of ancient oceans and the habitability of water worlds.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>新研究表明，长期被视为死水的缺氧极区或含有关于地球早期生命及其在低氧环境中如何生存的线索。相关发现可能改变我们对古代海洋及水世界潜在宜居性的认知。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(262) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://thehustle.co/originals/why-barcodes-are-about-to-go-extinct' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Barcodes are about to go extinct</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>条形码濒临消亡</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49938306' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 106</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49938306' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 150</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Barcodes are being displaced by QR codes, RFID, and other digital tagging technologies as scanning becomes faster and data richer. The piece traces the shift across retail, logistics, and manufacturing, and discusses what this means for supply chains, data analytics, and standardization.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>条形码正在被二维码、射频识别等数字标签技术取代，扫描速度与数据丰富度提升。文章追踪这一转变在零售、物流与制造业的影响，并讨论对供应链、数据分析与标准化的意义。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(266) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://pikuma.com/blog/c-integer-sizes-not-a-mistake' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>C&#x27;s Flexible Integer Sizes Were Not a Design Mistake</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49845114' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 102</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49845114' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 159</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that C&#x27;s varied integer widths were deliberate to balance portability, performance, and hardware realities. It reassures that size flexibility is a strength, not an accident.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章认为 C 语言的不同整数位宽是有意为之，用以在可移植性、性能和硬件现实之间取得平衡，强调这种灵活性是优点而非错误。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>外援中的加密货币捕获</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>NBER study examines how crypto channels can divert foreign aid, weakening oversight. It quantifies leakage paths and discusses policy remedies to preserve aid effectiveness while enabling legitimate crypto philanthropy.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>NBER 研究探讨加密货币渠道如何分流外国援助、削弱监督，量化可能的流失路径并讨论在促进合规慈善捐赠的同时维持援助效力的政策对策。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(272) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://sdf.org/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>SDF Public Access Unix System ... est. 1987</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>SDF 公共访问 UNIX 系统，成立于 1987 年</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49909610' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 97</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49909610' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 24</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>SDF Public Access UNIX System is one of the oldest community-run free Unix systems, established in 1987. It remains a living archive of classic tooling and a reminder of grassroots computing culture.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>SDF 公共访问 UNIX 系统是最早的社区运营免费 UNIX 系统之一，创建于 1987 年，至今仍在运行，成为经典工具集的活档案与草根计算文化的象征。</p>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>天赋与双重特殊才能儿童的拒绝敏感</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49953116' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 94</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49953116' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 50</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Rejection sensitivity is disproportionately high in gifted and twice-exceptional children, influencing motivation and social engagement. The piece offers actionable guidance for educators and parents to bolster resilience, reduce anxiety, and foster inclusive learning environments.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在天赋儿童与双重特殊才能儿童中，拒绝敏感往往更高，对学习动机和社交参与产生影响。文章提出面向教育者和家长的实用策略，帮助提升韧性、减轻焦虑，并营造包容的学习环境。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(278) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nytimes.com/2026/09/28/science/virus-molluscum-human-gene.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Virus Stole a Human Gene and Won&#x27;t Let Go of It</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>病毒窃取人类基因并不愿放弃</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49883635' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 92</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49883635' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 35</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers report a molluscum contagiosum–like virus has captured a human gene, integrating it into its own genome and effectively co opting it. The finding sheds light on viral gene capture and its potential implications for viral evolution and gene function.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究发现一种类似皮肤软疣病毒的病毒捕获了一个人类基因并将其整合进自身基因组，形成持续依赖。该发现揭示了病毒基因捕获的现象及其对病毒进化和基因功能的潜在影响。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Explores the physics and observation quirks around the lunar terminator, the line that marks day and night on the Moon. <p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>聚焦月球的日夜分界线，揭示其物理现象与观测中的趣味与难题。极端光照、阴影和地形效应给探测与制图带来意外挑战。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(283) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/deepfates/imp' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Imp is a full port of DSPy to the BEAM</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Imp 将 DSPy 完整移植到 BEAM</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49869995' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 86</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49869995' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 7</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Imp is a full port of DSPy to the BEAM VM, bringing DSPy’s data-science capabilities to Erlang and Elixir ecosystems. The project enables BEAM friendly probabilistic programming workflows and leverages the actor model for parallelism across cores.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Imp 将 DSPy 完整移植到 BEAM 虚拟机，将 DSPy 的数据科学能力带入 Erlang/Elixir 生态。该项目为 BEAM 上的概率编程和并行工作流提供更友好的实现，利用 actor 模型在多核上实现并行。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/6631cc39fbfabc161a38cecb6ea61ec33a70102e46a023a6a313b3170f5e277b/deepfates/imp' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/6631cc39fbfabc161a38cecb6ea61ec33a70102e46a023a6a313b3170f5e277b/deepfates/imp' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49856193' data-tags='Web'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(284) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.blog/engineering/architecture-optimization/improving-site-performance-by-shipping-more-css/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Improving site performance by shipping more CSS</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>通过投放更多 CSS 提升站点性能</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49856193' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 86</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49856193' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 69</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that increasing the amount of CSS delivered upfront can improve page speed by reducing render-blocking and extra network trips, especially when styles are reused across pages. It offers data-backed guidance on caching strategies, specificity, and bundle sizing to balance initial paint with repeat visits.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章认为在初始加载中投放更多 CSS 可以通过减少渲染阻塞和重复请求来提升页面速度，尤其是跨页面大量复用样式时。给出关于缓存策略、选择性和打包规模的权衡要点，帮助兼顾首屏和后续访问。</p>

</div>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A concise explainer of the -r vs -R debate in cp across Unix-like systems. The post contrasts historical differences, portability considerations, and practical implications for recursively copying directories and handling symlinks. The takeaway: prefer -R for portability and POSIX-aligned behavior.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>简要解释了 cp 命令中 -r 与 -R 的差异及在不同系统上的表现。文章对比了历史差异、可移植性考量以及在递归拷贝目录和处理符号链接时的实际影响。结论是为实现跨平台兼容性，宜优先使用 -R。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49883913' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 80</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49883913' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 6</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Demonstrates using Facet to test and compare WebGPU data layouts, highlighting memory alignment, padding, and stride effects on performance. The piece offers practical tips for choosing layouts to improve cache locality and bandwidth.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>演示了如何借助 Facet 测试并比较 WebGPU 的数据布局，揭示内存对齐、填充和步幅对性能的影响。文中给出实用建议，帮助开发者在缓存局部性与带宽之间做出平衡，选择更高效的布局。</p>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(298) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/Vibra-Ingenn/Janus' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Janus – Go binary that runs GGUF models via Vulkan on AMD/Intel/Nvidia</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN: Janus – Go binary that runs GGUF models via Vulkan on AMD/Intel/Nvidia</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49926773' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 80</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49926773' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 14</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Janus is a Go-based binary that runs GGUF-formatted models via Vulkan across major GPUs, enabling local AI inference with a single cross-platform tool. It streamlines setup, supports multiple backends, and lowers the barrier for experiments on consumer hardware.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Janus 是一个用 Go 编写的二进制程序，借助 Vulkan 在 AMD/Intel/Nvidia 等显卡上运行 GGUF 模型，实现本地推理。它简化了安装流程，支持多种后端，并降低了在消费级硬件上进行实验的门槛。</p>

</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(306) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://joshua.hu/magic-switch-easily-switch-magic-keyboard-trackpad-mouse-between-mac-macbook-macos' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Magic Switch: Share Apple Magic keyboard/track-pad/mouse between two Macs</a></p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>One Apple Magic keyboard, trackpad, and mouse can be shared between two Macs with a simple setup, streamlining a two-computer workflow. The technique reduces clutter and keeps input devices in sync across machines.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>通过简单设置，一组 Apple Magic 键盘、触控板和鼠标即可在两台 Mac 之间共享，简化双机工作流。该方法让输入设备在两台机器之间无缝切换，减少桌面凌乱。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(307) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/mogrinz/AMT630A' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Reverse-engineering a $35 backup camera display (AMT630A)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>一个价值 35 美元的倒车摄像头显示屏的逆向工程（AMT630A）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49884346' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 75</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49884346' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 25</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A hardware reverse-engineering deep-dive uncovers the internal controller AMT630A used in cheap backup camera displays, revealing how to repurpose or replace it. It offers practical insights into sourcing affordable components and understanding display interfaces.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对一款价值约 35 美元的倒车摄像头显示屏进行逆向工程，揭示了其内部控制芯片 AMT630A 的工作原理与接口。提供了利用廉价部件进行再利用的实用洞见。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(319) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://jonclegg.github.io/pacman-bakeoff/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Pac-Bench – How well can models one-shot a Pac-Man game?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Pac-Bench：模型能否一次性完成吃豆人游戏？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49885493' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 69</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49885493' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 44</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Gaming'>Gaming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Pac-Bench benchmarks how well models can learn to play Pac-Man from minimal, one-shot experiences. The project tests few-shot generalization and memory in game-playing agents, offering a playful gauge of current AI&#x27;s sample efficiency.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Pac-Bench 测试模型是否能通过极少量、一次性经验来学习并玩 Pac-Man。该项目评估游戏智能体的少样本泛化能力与记忆能力，提供对当前 AI 样本效率的有趣量化。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Jane Street 公布长期 ASIC 谜题的最新结果，揭示定制硬件在何时真正具备性价比，以及算法与工具链如何影响硬件开发的选择。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Explains the Ballmer Peak meme—the idea that programmer output peaks at a specific alcohol range—and its place in tech culture. The piece cautions against turning it into technical guidance and discusses why humor endures in software lore.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>解释 Ballmer 峰值这一梗，指程序员产出在特定酒精范围内达到巅峰的说法及其在科技文化中的地位。文章提醒不要把它当成技术指南，并讨论幽默在软件文化中的持续存在原因。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(325) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.google/innovation-and-ai/models-and-research/google-research/project-suncatcher-prototype/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Our Project Suncatcher prototype satellite is in orbit</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49932191' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 67</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49932191' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 65</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Our Project Suncatcher prototype satellite is now in orbit, marking a milestone for space research and on‑orbit experimentation. The mission demonstrates the feasibility of lightweight, iterative hardware tests and embedded AI capabilities in a small satellite.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>我们的 Project Suncatcher 原型卫星已入轨，标志着太空研究与在轨实验的重要里程碑。该任务展示了轻量级、可迭代的硬件测试以及在小型卫星中运行的人工智能能力的可行性。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>GitHub has rolled out its redesigned dashboard as the default experience, signaling a shift in how developers get a project overview. The new interface emphasizes a centralized activity feed, quicker navigation, and modular widgets for issues, PRs, and repos. Users may need to adapt to the updated layout and new features as they settle in.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>GitHub 将全新改版的仪表板设为默认体验，改变开发者获取项目概览的方式。新界面强调集中式活动摘要、快速导航，以及用于问题、合并请求和仓库的模块化小部件。用户需要适应更新的布局和新功能带来的变化。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Halide blog presents memory-safe WebP decoding, detailing techniques to prevent common vulnerabilities in image decoders. By applying bounds-aware decoding and safer memory management, the approach aims to reduce exploits without sacrificing decoding performance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Halide 博客介绍了内存安全的 WebP 解码，阐述了防范图像解码器常见漏洞的技术思路。通过边界感知解码和更安全的内存管理，力求在不牺牲解码性能的前提下降低安全风险。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Argues that S3 remains the lingua franca of cloud storage and will influence future architecture, even as new storage approaches emerge.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>认为 S3 仍是云存储的通用语言，未来架构将持续受其影响，即使涌现出新的存储方案。文章分析了 S3 API 的普及为何会持续主导开发者与运营者的选择，以及对行业的影响。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49940482' data-tags='Education'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(331) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=UOuxo6SA8Uc' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Math&#x27;s pedagogical curse – Grant Sanderson [video] (2023)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>数学的教学诅咒 – Grant Sanderson [视频]</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49940482' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 64</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49940482' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 33</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Grant Sanderson argues that math education is hamstrung by pedagogical biases that favor procedures over understanding. The video presents visual intuition and progressive concepts as a remedy to rote learning, aiming to make math feel approachable and enduring. It challenges educators to rethink how diagrams and storytelling can illuminate concepts.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Grant Sanderson 指出数学教育常被公式和程序的偏见所拖累，理解被抑制。视频提出以直观视觉和渐进性概念来取代机械记忆，帮助学生建立更深层的数学直觉。此举旨在推动教师通过可视化与直觉方法来阐释概念，减少死记硬背。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>gpuvis turns GPU trace logs into interactive timelines, helping developers pinpoint where rendering or compute bottlenecks occur. By visualizing GPU activity, it simplifies performance tuning across graphics pipelines.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>gpuvis 将 GPU 跟踪日志转化为交互式时间线，帮助开发者定位渲染或计算瓶颈。通过直观的可视化，简化了图形管线的性能调优。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>用于室内能量收集与湿度管理的湿电壁纸</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49910613' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 63</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49910613' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 37</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Researchers propose a wallpaper that harvests energy from ambient humidity to power low-power electronics, coupling humidity-driven electrochemical processes with flexible substrates. It hints at near-permanent IoT devices with minimal maintenance, while noting efficiency and durability challenges.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究团队提出一种可从环境湿度中收集能量的湿电壁纸，利用湿度驱动的电化学过程在柔性基材上发电。该技术有望为低功耗物联网设备提供长期供电，但也面临效率与耐久性挑战。</p>
</div>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 作弊? 加入“请勿猜测”限制将编造声明的比例从71%降至20%</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49868753' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 63</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49868753' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 14</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Reports that instructing AI not to guess dramatically reduces made-up claims in benchmarks, showing how simple prompt design can cut hallucinations. It highlights the importance of guardrails and robust evaluation when deploying AI.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>显示在 AI 系统中加入“请勿猜测”指令后，生成虚假陈述的比例显著下降，证明简单的提示设计也能显著提升安全性与可靠性。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(341) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.lesswrong.com/posts/Nm4ewbYovtjq69dvH/pacing-the-frontier-is-not-the-actual-goal-for-ai-labs' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Pacing the Frontier is not the actual goal for AI labs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>‘把控前沿节奏’并非 AI 实验室的真正目标</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49884119' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 61</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49884119' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 52</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The essay argues that AI labs should not frame progress as simply &#x27;pacing the frontier&#x27;; instead, prioritize safety, reliability, governance, and real-world value to avoid reckless speed and misaligned systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这篇文章认为 AI 实验室不应把“把控前沿节奏”作为唯一目标，而应优先考虑安全性、可靠性与治理，以及对现实世界的价值，以避免过快推进带来的风险。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://res.cloudinary.com/lesswrong-2-0/image/upload/f_auto,q_auto/v1/mirroredImages/Nm4ewbYovtjq69dvH/kyuppql2bwtjsypsuj3s' data-full='https://res.cloudinary.com/lesswrong-2-0/image/upload/f_auto,q_auto/v1/mirroredImages/Nm4ewbYovtjq69dvH/kyuppql2bwtjsypsuj3s' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49939637' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(342) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://math.tejstead.com/heilbronn/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The Heilbronn Problem</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>海尔布龙问题</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49939637' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 61</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49939637' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 12</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Heilbronn problem asks how to place N points inside a unit triangle to minimize the largest empty disk, a classic question in computational geometry. The write-up surveys known bounds and recent progress, with implications for mesh generation and numerical methods.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>海尔布龙问题是关于在单位三角形内放置 N 点以最小化最大空圆半径的经典几何问题。文章回顾已知界限与最近进展，并讨论其对网格生成和数值方法的含义。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://math.tejstead.com/heilbronn/og.png' data-full='https://math.tejstead.com/heilbronn/og.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49851795' data-tags='Programming'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(343) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=25x0UuSCKuU' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Deterministic Concurrency [video]</a></p>

<p class='hn-title'>(355) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://rhun.app/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Rhun, an open-source code editor written in assembly</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49926726' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 55</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49926726' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 32</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Rhun is an open-source code editor implemented in assembly, trading portability for raw speed and a tiny footprint. It showcases a bold experiment in building a productive editor from the metal up, inviting community contributions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Rhun 是一个用汇编实现的开源代码编辑器，追求极致的速度和极小的占用。它是一次大胆的从底层出发的编辑器实践，欢迎社区贡献。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49898931' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 52</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49898931' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 10</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>TurboGPT claims to train a 22KiB transformer in 13 seconds, challenging conventional notions of data and compute needs for even tiny models. The project invites discussion about the boundaries between toy experiments and practical, scalable training techniques.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>TurboGPT 声称在 13 秒内训练出一个 22KiB 的 Transformer，挑战了即便是微型模型也需大量数据和算力的常识。该项目促使人们思考 toy 级实验与可扩展训练方法之间的界限。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49909648' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 49</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49909648' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 23</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Lathoa is a math app for kids that deliberately presents incorrect AI responses to teach users about error detection and debugging. The project frames mistakes as educational prompts, encouraging critical thinking and resilience in young learners.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Lathoa 是一个面向儿童的数学应用，故意给出错误的 AI 回答以训练用户发现并修正错误的能力。通过把错误当作学习线索，鼓励年轻用户进行批判性思维和韧性训练。</p>
</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49957117' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 47</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49957117' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 14</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Homa presents a datacenter transport designed for AI clusters that aims to outperform TCP in latency and throughput. The video outlines how this approach could change inter-node communication during large-scale model training and inference.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Homa 提供为 AI 集群定制的数据中心传输方案，目标是在延迟和吞吐量方面优于传统 TCP。视频展示了该方案在大规模模型训练与推理中的潜在影响。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49883159' data-tags='Security,Data'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(380) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://blog.plcred.org/3mwlphq42d227' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>First Steps of the PLC Organization – Independent Public Ledger of Credentials</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>PLC 组织的初步进展：独立凭证公账</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49883159' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 46</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49883159' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 21</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The Independent Public Ledger of Credentials aims to create an auditable, independent record of credentials to curb fraud. Early steps discuss governance, architecture, and potential adoption by institutions and individuals.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>独立公证凭证总账意在打造一个可审计的独立凭证记录，抑制伪造。初步阶段聚焦治理、体系架构以及机构和个人的潜在采纳。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://leaflet.pub/lish/did%3Aplc%3Ai65vdx4ylh5ahdjyd7f2gpnp/3mukis7uxvs2a/3mwlphq42d227/opengraph-image-1qv6ug?c1d2f6c379aabdcf' data-full='https://leaflet.pub/lish/did%3Aplc%3Ai65vdx4ylh5ahdjyd7f2gpnp/3mukis7uxvs2a/3mwlphq42d227/opengraph-image-1qv6ug?c1d2f6c379aabdcf' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<p class='hn-title'>(381) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://sigh.dev/posts/metas-muse-is-fantastic-for-web-scraping/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Meta&#x27;s Muse is fantastic for web scraping</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Meta的Muse在网页抓取方面表现出色</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49929970' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 46</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49929970' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 53</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Meta&#x27;s Muse is a powerful tool for web scraping, offering integrated browsing and data extraction that can speed up research workflows. It raises considerations around ethics, rate limits, and data provenance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Meta 的 Muse 是一个强大的网页抓取工具，集成浏览和数据提取，能显著提速研究流程。使用时需关注伦理、速率限制和数据出处等问题。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>这就是关于杂质的故事</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49926609' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 46</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49926609' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 4</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Science blog frames impurities as a core driver of materials science stories, highlighting how small defects can enable big breakthroughs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>科学博客把杂质视为材料科学故事的核心驱动，强调微小缺陷也能带来重大突破。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49922074' data-tags='Hardware,Design,Energy'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(383) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.aboutamazon.com/news/devices/new-kindle-lineup-2026' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Amazon introduces a redesigned Kindle family</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>亚马逊推出全新设计的 Kindle 家族</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49922074' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 46</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49922074' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 84</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Amazon unveils a redesigned Kindle lineup with refreshed hardware and software, aiming to improve reading experience, battery life, and accessibility while keeping the paper-like display.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>亚马逊推出重新设计的 Kindle 系列，改善阅读体验、续航和无障碍功能，同时延续纸感显示的优势，以应对竞争。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49889633' data-tags='Startups'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(384) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.startup-nights.ch/event/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Startup Nights 2026 is comming up on 5-6 Nov. in Switzerland</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Startup Nights 2026 将于11月5-6日于瑞士举行</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49889633' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 45</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49889633' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 26</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Startups'>Startups</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Startup Nights 2026 in Switzerland brings together startups, investors, and researchers for two days of talks, demos, and networking. The event spotlights Europe’s vibrant early-stage ecosystem and cross-border collaboration.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>瑞士将于11月5-6日举行 Startup Nights 2026，聚集初创公司、投资人和研究者进行两天的演讲、路演和社交。活动聚焦欧洲充满活力的早期生态系统与跨境合作。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.startup-nights.ch/wp-content/uploads/2025/04/10YearLogoSN_yellow-scaled-e1779952130536-300x92.png' data-full='https://www.startup-nights.ch/wp-content/uploads/2025/04/10YearLogoSN_yellow-scaled-e1779952130536-300x92.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49881692' data-tags='Culture,Politics'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(385) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.smithsonianmag.com/history/who-wrote-elizabeth-is-most-scathing-letters-new-research-suggests-the-tudor-queens-male-secretaries-revised-her-correspondence-to-emphasize-her-temper-180989565/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Who wrote Elizabeth I&#x27;s most scathing letters?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>谁写了伊丽莎白一世最尖刻的信？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49881692' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 45</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49881692' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 22</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>New archival work suggests Elizabeth I’s sharp letters were edited or revised by male secretaries to amplify temper and authority. The finding reframes how we read the queen’s voice in correspondence and the politics of authorship.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>最新考古发现表明，伊丽莎白一世的尖刻信件可能经由男性秘书修改以放大脾气与权威。这一发现改变了我们对女王书信声音的解读以及署名权力的政治。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://th-thumbnailer.cdn-si-edu.com/d5hXq7IqkVcRFN7KUNHUBbdUo4M=/fit-in/1600x0/filters:focal(750x500:751x501)/https://tf-cmsv2-smithsonianmag-media.s3.amazonaws.com/filer_public/41/c0/41c011a5-551b-4c52-a8c6-675129038507/elizabeth1illo.png' data-full='https://th-thumbnailer.cdn-si-edu.com/d5hXq7IqkVcRFN7KUNHUBbdUo4M=/fit-in/1600x0/filters:focal(750x500:751x501)/https://tf-cmsv2-smithsonianmag-media.s3.amazonaws.com/filer_public/41/c0/41c011a5-551b-4c52-a8c6-675129038507/elizabeth1illo.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49872474' data-tags='Culture'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(386) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.historyofinformation.com/detail.php?entryid=4341' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Was silent reading unusual during Augustine&#x27;s time?</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>圣奥古斯丁时代默读是否罕见？</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49872474' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 45</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49872474' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 19</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Historical analysis questions whether silent reading was common or unusual in Augustine’s era, challenging assumptions about ancient literacy and oral culture. The piece sketches how reading practices coexisted with or diverged from spoken culture in late antiquity.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对圣奥古斯丁时期默读是否罕见进行历史考证，挑战对古代读写方式的普遍假设。研究描绘了晚期古代的阅读习惯如何与口头文化共存或互异。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.historyofinformation.com/assets/coinlogo2.png' data-full='https://www.historyofinformation.com/assets/coinlogo2.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49921825' data-tags='Hardware,AI'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(387) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.oki.com/global/press/2025/z25006e.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>OKI Develops 124-Layer PCB Technology</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>OKI 发布 124 层 PCB 技术</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49921825' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 45</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49921825' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 23</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>OKI unveils a 124-layer PCB technology, signaling advances in HDI manufacturing that enable denser, faster, and more compact electronics. These ultra-layer boards can support high-density interconnects for next-generation AI hardware and complex devices.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>OKI 发布了 124 层 PCB 技术，标志着高密度互连制造的进步，能够实现更紧凑且性能更高的电子设备。如此高层数的板材有望支撑下一代 AI 硬件与复杂系统的高级互连。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.oki.com/global/assets/images/opengraph-image.png' data-full='https://www.oki.com/global/assets/images/opengraph-image.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49932048' data-tags='Culture,Entertainment'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(388) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theguardian.com/media/2026/oct/02/physical-media-fans-streaming-algorithms-cds-dvds-vinyl' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Why media fans want to escape algorithms with CDs, DVDs and vinyl</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>媒体粉丝为何用CD、DVD与黑胶逃离算法</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49932048' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 45</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49932048' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 45</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Entertainment'>Entertainment</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Physical media persists as a countercurrent to algorithmic curation, as fans seek ownership, tangibility, and stable listening experiences. CDs, DVDs, and vinyl offer predictable formats and often superior sound quality, while reducing exposure to platform-driven recommendations and data collection. The trend underscores enduring demand for material media in an age of personalized algorithms.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>物理介质在算法推荐主导的时代仍有市场。CD、DVD和黑胶让粉丝获得实物所有权、可控的听觉体验，以及更稳定的收藏价值，避免被平台的数据收集和推荐影响。此趋势凸显了在个性化算法盛行的背景下，实体媒介的持续需求。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/03273be0ebbd56d8c423c5ea1508c74032bd291c/844_0_5664_4530/master/5664.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=79f6126e28345b627cb23542881aa4f0' data-full='https://i.guim.co.uk/img/media/03273be0ebbd56d8c423c5ea1508c74032bd291c/844_0_5664_4530/master/5664.jpg?width=1200&amp;height=630&amp;quality=85&amp;auto=format&amp;fit=crop&amp;precrop=40:21,offset-x50,offset-y0&amp;overlay-align=bottom%2Cleft&amp;overlay-width=100p&amp;overlay-base64=L2ltZy9zdGF0aWMvb3ZlcmxheXMvdGctZGVmYXVsdC5wbmc&amp;enable=upscale&amp;s=79f6126e28345b627cb23542881aa4f0' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49863594' data-tags='Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(389) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://countingfromzero.blog/2026/04/24/four-chi-26-papers-i-wish-i-wrote/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Four CHI &#x27;26 papers I wish I wrote</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49863594' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 44</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49863594' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 10</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A personal roundup of four CHI 2026 papers the author wishes they had authored, highlighting standout ideas in human-computer interaction and takeaways for readers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>作者列出 CHI ’26 的四篇论文，表示希望自己也写过，反映出人机交互领域的亮点观点及对读者的启发。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://s0.wp.com/i/blank.jpg?m=1383295312i' data-full='https://s0.wp.com/i/blank.jpg?m=1383295312i' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49951891' data-tags='AI,Design'>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49913106' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(395) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.nature.com/articles/s41598-026-57500-7' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Body Awareness in Goffin&#x27;s Cockatoos</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>戈芬氏葵鹦鹉的身体意识研究</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49913106' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 41</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49913106' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 4</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>New findings show that Goffin&#x27;s cockatoos exhibit body awareness, suggesting they understand their own bodies in space. This adds to growing evidence of sophisticated cognition in birds, challenging assumptions about animal self-awareness.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>最新研究显示戈芬氏葵鹦鹉具备身体意识，能够在空间中理解自身位置。这为鸟类认知的复杂性提供新证据，挑战了关于动物自我意识的既有假设。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://media.springernature.com/m685/springer-static/image/art%3A10.1038%2Fs41598-026-57500-7/MediaObjects/41598_2026_57500_Fig1_HTML.png' data-full='https://media.springernature.com/m685/springer-static/image/art%3A10.1038%2Fs41598-026-57500-7/MediaObjects/41598_2026_57500_Fig1_HTML.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49934399' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 41</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49934399' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 14</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Questioning the calibration of Jev, the author argues the model&#x27;s predictions are poorly aligned with real-world outcomes, undermining trust in its outputs.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>质疑 Jev 的校准程度，认为模型的预测与实际结果之间存在明显偏差，影响输出可信度。文章还讨论校准误差在关键决策型 AI 应用中的传播风险与应对要点。</p>

</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(397) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://journals.sagepub.com/doi/10.1177/23998083261458842' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>What is the best shape of a city? Modelling effect of urban form on distance</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>城市的最佳形状是什么？对距离的城市形态建模</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49858643' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 39</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49858643' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 24</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A study modeling how city shape affects travel distance, accessibility, and land use, shedding light on the rail/road network design and urban planning tradeoffs between density and sprawl. Implications for reducing commute times and emissions.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>一项建模研究揭示城市形状如何影响通勤距离、可达性和土地利用，探讨密度与扩张之间的取舍，以及轨道与公路网络的设计要点。对缩短通勤时间和降低排放具有现实意义。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49938326' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 39</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49938326' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Explores how multilingual AI agents operate across different regulatory and linguistic environments, revealing how the web becomes uneven due to governance, access, and data sovereignty.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>本文讨论多语言 AI 代理在不同监管和语言环境中的运作，揭示网络因治理、接入和数据主权而呈现不平等。指出跨区域的互操作性挑战，以及 AI 行为可能因此产生差异的风险，强调建立全球标准和包容性设计的必要性。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(403) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/net4people/bbs/issues/672' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Xray-core concealed a certificate verification bypass vulnerability</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Xray-core 隐藏的证书验证漏洞</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49956003' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 38</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49956003' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 0</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Xray-core contains a certificate verification bypass vulnerability that could allow man-in-the-middle attacks. The finding underscores the importance of verifying all certificate handling in proxy software and applying patches.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Xray-core 存在一个证书验证绕过漏洞，可能被用来执行中间人攻击。这一发现强调了代理软件中对证书处理的严格验证和及时打补丁的重要性。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(404) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.getprivisy.com/blog/sb-923-ccpa-right-to-delete-signed' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>SB 923 is Law: CCPA deletion rights now reach third-party data</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>SB 923 成法：CCPA 删除权扩展至第三方数据</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49883345' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 37</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49883345' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 12</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>SB 923 broadens CCPA deletion rights to data held by third parties, expanding consumer control and imposing new compliance burdens on processors and data brokers. Expect policy debates and new tooling for data deletion workflows.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>SB 923 将删除权扩展至第三方数据，扩大了消费者对自身数据的控制，并给数据处理者与数据经纪商带来新的合规负担。未来或引发合规工具与流程的升级。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(405) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.red5.net/blog/mpeg-2-transport-streams-and-moq-yes-mpeg-ts-is-still-relevant-today/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>MPEG-2 Transport Streams and MOQ: Yes, MPEG-TS Is Still Relevant Today</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>MPEG-2 传输流与 MOQ：是的，MPEG-TS 仍然相关</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49923602' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 37</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49923602' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 9</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues MPEG-TS remains relevant for modern workflows, highlighting interoperability with legacy equipment, streaming pipelines, and broadcast use cases. It clarifies common misconceptions about the format&#x27;s obsolescence.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章认为 MPEG-TS 仍然在现代工作流中有用，强调与旧设备的互操作性、流媒体管线以及广播应用场景。还澄清了关于该格式普遍被误解的观点。</p>

It emphasizes standard interfaces, tooling discovery, and interoperability across agents. The project addresses a bottleneck in building scalable autonomous AI systems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Aweb 提供了一个让 AI 代理之间进行通信与协作的框架，支持更为一致的多代理工作流。它强调标准接口、工具发现与跨代理的互操作性。此举有助于构建可扩展的自治 AI 系统。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Early findings from the NTSB on the Prime Air 767 runway overrun emphasize testing of braking performance, runway conditions, and approach procedures; the report will shape safety recommendations for cargo aviation and avionics systems.</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(415) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/JoasASantos/Offensive-Security-AI-Models' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Uncensored and Offensive Security AI Models Benchmark</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49888937' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 33</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49888937' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 9</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A benchmark evaluating uncensored and offensive security AI models, highlighting safety risks, potential misuse, and the trade-offs between capability and responsible use.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对无审查与攻击性安全 AI 模型的基准测试，揭示安全风险、滥用可能性，以及能力与负责任使用之间的权衡。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49956035' data-tags='Science,Education'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(416) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.msoos.org/2026/10/incentives-in-academic-research/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Incentives in Academic Research</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>学术研究中的激励机制</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49956035' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 33</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49956035' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 15</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>This piece analyzes how incentives shape academic research, including publish-or-perish pressures, funding biases, and reproducibility challenges. It discusses how incentive structures affect what gets studied and how results are reported.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章分析学术研究中的激励机制如何影响研究方向与产出，包括刊出压力、资助偏好与可重复性挑战。讨论激励结构对研究主题与结果表达的影响。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='http://www.msoos.org/wordpress/wp-content/uploads/2026/10/stalker.jpg' data-full='http://www.msoos.org/wordpress/wp-content/uploads/2026/10/stalker.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(417) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://bencology.bearblog.dev/mapping-individual-and-collective-worm-movements/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>On building a worm detector</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>构建蠕虫探测器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49932509' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 32</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49932509' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 6</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece outlines approaches for mapping individual and collective worm movements, showing how simple sensors and data pipelines reveal swarm-like dynamics. Insights here inform soil ecology and bio-inspired design.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章概述了映射单体与群体蠕虫运动的做法，展示了简单传感器与数据管线如何揭示群体化的动态。相关见解有助于理解土壤生态与生物启发设计。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(418) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/STS-51-F' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>STS-51-F Abort-to-Orbit (1985)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>STS-51-F 中止入轨（1985）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49935797' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 32</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49935797' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 10</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Space'>Space</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>STS-51-F aborted before reaching orbit due to technical issues, a reminder of the dangers of spaceflight and the importance of rigorous safety protocols.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>STS-51-F 在发射后未进入轨道因技术问题中止，凸显航天飞行的风险与对严格安全规范的依赖。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://thumb.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/d/db/STS-51-F_Instrument_Pointing_System.jpg/1280px-STS-51-F_Instrument_Pointing_System.jpg?utm_source=en.wikipedia.org&amp;utm_campaign=index&amp;utm_content=thumbnail' data-full='https://thumb.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/d/db/STS-51-F_Instrument_Pointing_System.jpg/1280px-STS-51-F_Instrument_Pointing_System.jpg?utm_source=en.wikipedia.org&amp;utm_campaign=index&amp;utm_content=thumbnail' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(419) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theverge.com/column/1000778/smart-home-june-oven-graveyard' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The smart home graveyard is getting crowded</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>智能家居坟场越来越拥挤</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49876373' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 31</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49876373' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 23</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The smart-home landscape is crowded with devices that quickly become obsolete or unsupported, leaving a growing graveyard of gadgets. The piece argues for durable hardware, repairability, and open ecosystems to reduce waste.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>智能家居生态中设备易过时或失去支持，导致越来越多的“墓园”设备堆积。文章呼吁提升硬件耐用性、可维修性和开放生态，以减少浪费。</p>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(420) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.economist.com/business/2026/09/27/european-industry-is-doing-better-than-you-may-think' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>European industry is doing better than you may think</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>欧洲工业其实比你想象的要好</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49871992' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 31</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49871992' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 5</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>European industry shows more resilience and modernization than many narratives suggest, with productivity and policy factors shaping recovery. The piece analyzes energy costs, regulation and global competitiveness.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>欧洲工业虽然常被低估，但在生产率、现代化和政策环境方面表现好于部分评估。分析能源成本、法规与全球竞争格局对复苏的影响。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
