<p class='hn-title'>(421) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://chillphysicsenjoyer.substack.com/p/a-new-experiment-meta-strategy' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A New Experiment Meta-Strategy</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>一种新的实验元策略</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49844266' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 31</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49844266' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Proposes a meta-strategy for experiments that maximizes information gain under limits like time and budget. The approach aims to improve reproducibility and decision-making in research by structuring iterative testing.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>提出一套以信息增益最大化为目标的实验元策略，帮助研究者在时间、经费等约束下更高效地进行实验。该方法旨在提升可复现性和决策质量。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49931725' data-tags='Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(422) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://gizmodo.com/scientists-invent-underwater-umbrellas-to-protect-coral-reefs-and-it-appears-to-be-working-2000819444' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Scientists invent underwater umbrellas to protect coral reefs</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>科学家发明水下雨伞保护珊瑚礁</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49931725' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 31</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49931725' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 12</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Underwater umbrellas are deployed to shade reefs and mitigate thermal stress, showing early signs of reducing coral bleaching. This adds a new tool to coral conservation and climate-adaptation strategies.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>水下雨伞用于遮蔽珊瑚礁、降低热应力，初步迹象显示有助于降低珊瑚白化。这为珊瑚保护和气候适应策略提供了新的工具。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49937387' data-tags='Design,Hardware'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(423) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.greenend.org.uk/rjk/misc/nipple.html' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&quot;The only intuitive interface is the nipple&quot; (2012)</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>“唯一直观的界面是乳头”（2012）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49937387' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 31</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49937387' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 34</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--pink' data-tag='Design'>Design</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A provocative line from 2012 arguing that tactile interaction remains the most intuitive interface, highlighting the limits of screen-centric UX. It prompts reflection on when hardware feedback should drive design decisions in interface design.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>2012 年的一句挑衅性论断指出触觉交互仍是最直观的界面，揭示了以屏幕为中心的用户体验的局限。引发人们对在界面设计中何时应让硬件反馈主导决策的思考。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49889275' data-tags='Politics,Security'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(424) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.reuters.com/world/middle-east/us-forces-exit-iraq-after-two-decades-leaving-opening-iran-2026-09-29/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>US Forces Exit Iraq</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>美军撤离伊拉克</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49889275' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 30</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49889275' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 15</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>After two decades, US forces complete a staged withdrawal from Iraq, reshaping the regional security landscape. The move reduces direct American military footprint while potentially widening space for Iran-backed groups and complicating Iraqi security coordination.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>美军结束长达二十年的在伊拉克部署，完成阶段性撤离，显著改变中东区域安全格局。随着直接军事存在的缩减，伊朗支持的力量可能获得更大空间，同时对伊拉克安全协作也带来新的挑战。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>这是关于埃隆·马斯克邮件的收藏，提供对他在各家企业的决策思维与沟通风格的洞见。整理展示了领导力与战略思考在实际沟通中的体现。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49927295' data-tags='Show HN,Programming,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(426) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/graphene-data/graphene' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Graphene – Data analysis toolkit for your coding agent</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN: Graphene – 为你的编码代理提供的数据分析工具包</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49927295' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 30</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49927295' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 9</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Graphene is a data-analysis toolkit tailored for coding agents, enabling structured data processing, experimentation, and debugging. It emphasizes seamless integration with autonomous agents and improving their data-driven decision-making.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Graphene 为你的编码代理提供数据分析工具包，支持结构化数据处理、实验与调试。文中强调了与自主代理的无缝集成以及提升数据驱动决策能力的点。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49927514' data-tags='Hardware,Design'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>

<p class='hn-title'>(428) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.404media.co/federal-judge-rules-a-flock-search-was-indiscriminate-mass-surveillance-and-unconstitutional/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Federal Judge Rules a Flock Search Was Unconstitutional</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>联邦法官裁定 Flock 搜索违反宪法</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49938815' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 30</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49938815' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 4</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A federal judge ruled that a Flock surveillance search was unconstitutional due to its indiscriminate scope and privacy concerns. The decision adds a notable privacy safeguard against broad, automated data collection.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>联邦法官裁定 Flock 的监控搜索因范围过广且侵犯隐私而违宪。这一裁决为对广泛、自动化数据收集的隐私保护再添一枚重要砝码。</p>

</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(429) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://vincent.bernat.ch/en/blog/2026-http-over-ssh' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Self-hosted HTTP tunnels with SSH and Nginx</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>使用 SSH 与 Nginx 构建自托管的 HTTP 隧道</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49958569' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 30</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49958569' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 10</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Self-hosted HTTP tunnels route traffic via SSH to a public endpoint, with Nginx acting as the reverse proxy to expose internal services. This approach offers greater privacy and control, but relies on SSH access and careful configuration.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>自托管的 HTTP 隧道通过 SSH 将流量转发到公网端点，并以 Nginx 作为反向代理来暴露内部服务。这种做法提升了隐私和控制力，但需要 SSH 访问权限并需谨慎配置。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://d2pzklc15kok91.cloudfront.net/images/covers/en/blog/2026-http-over-ssh.922a043bebfeb0.jpg' data-full='https://d2pzklc15kok91.cloudfront.net/images/covers/en/blog/2026-http-over-ssh.922a043bebfeb0.jpg' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(431) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://neow.in/NGxhb3d6' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Your Big Mac might cost more if McDonald&#x27;s AI thinks people nearby can afford it</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>如果麦当劳周边的人买得起，它的巨无霸价格可能更高</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49927693' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 30</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49927693' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 28</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>McDonald&#x27;s experiments with AI-driven dynamic pricing based on local affordability, meaning near-by customers may see higher menu prices. The approach aims to optimize revenue but raises concerns about fairness and price discrimination. It underscores how AI can inject locality-aware pricing into everyday consumer experiences.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>麦当劳正在测试基于本地购买力的 AI 动态定价，附近顾客的价格可能上升。此做法旨在提升收入，但也引发关于公平性和价格歧视的担忧。它凸显 AI 将本地化定价引入日常消费场景的现实影响。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(434) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.sanders.senate.gov/press-releases/news-sanders-ocasio-cortez-merkley-unveil-ban-flock-act-to-protect-americans-right-to-privacy/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Ban Flock Act Proposed in the US Sente</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Ban Flock Act 提议在美国参议院</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49947176' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 29</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49947176' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 0</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Politics'>Politics</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Senators propose the Ban Flock Act to curb surveillance tech used by private contractors and public agencies, aiming to protect privacy and civil liberties. The bill would restrict deployment and data-sharing practices of certain camera networks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>参议员提出《Ban Flock Act》，以限制私人承包商和公共机构使用的监控技术，保护隐私与公民自由。该法案将对某些摄像头网络的部署与数据共享行为设定限制。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.sanders.senate.gov/wp-content/uploads/2020/11/bernie-sanders-logo-greens-yellow-768x116.png' data-full='https://www.sanders.senate.gov/wp-content/uploads/2020/11/bernie-sanders-logo-greens-yellow-768x116.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(435) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Blindsight_(Watts_novel)' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Blindsight (Watts Novel)</a></p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Blindsight is a hard sci-fi novel that pushes hard on questions of consciousness and intelligence through a first-contact scenario with an alien entity. It explores perception, cognition, and the ethics of technology in a future where biology, computation, and interface converge.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>《盲视》是一部硬科幻小说，通过与外星生命体的首次接触，深挖意识、认知与智能的边界。作品探讨感知、认知与技术伦理，以及生物、计算与接口在未来的交织。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(437) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.reddit.com/r/GeminiAI/comments/1wwalmc/wtf_google_getting_rid_of_free_gemini_flash_and/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Gemini ending free use of Flash and Pro models</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Gemini 将结束对 Flash 与 Pro 模型的免费使用</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49942592' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 28</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49942592' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 15</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Business'>Business</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Google Gemini is ending free access to its Flash and Pro models, signaling a shift to paid tiers for some capabilities. The change will affect startups and individuals relying on free access to AI models and may push users toward paid plans or alternative providers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Google Gemini 将停止对 Flash 与 Pro 模型提供免费使用，转向付费访问。此举可能影响依赖免费额度进行开发的初创公司和个人，可能促使用户转向付费方案或其他厂商。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>愚蠢的专业知识</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49872719' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 26</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49872719' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 13</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--rose' data-tag='Culture'>Culture</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Fool&#x27;s Expertise critiques the appeal of presumed tech mastery, arguing that expertise is often overestimated and context-dependent. The piece advocates focusing on testable results, humility, and continuous learning.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>评析对被认为具备专业知识的科技从业者的盲信，指出专业能力往往被高估且情境依赖。倡导以可验证结果、谦逊与持续学习为核心的能力观。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(447) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/edgcpp/compiler' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>EDG C++ Compiler is open source</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>EDG C++ 编译器开源</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49915484' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 25</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49915484' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 1</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--cyan' data-tag='Web'>Web</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>EDG&#x27;s C++ compiler frontend has been open-sourced on GitHub, giving researchers and tool builders access to a mature parsing pipeline that underpins many commercial toolchains. The move lowers barriers for experimentation with C++ frontends, static analysis, and language tooling, and could influence compiler ecosystem dynamics.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>EDG 的 C++ 编译器前端已在 GitHub 开源，开发者和研究者现在可以访问成熟的解析管线，其广泛支撑着多家商用工具链。此举降低了对 C++ 前端、静态分析和语言工具开发的门槛，可能影响编译器生态的演进与竞争格局。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/9ca5f232d1d58ec7d7c599dfedf743f26c504fd5dac349d433fc6e7b6fa1a9d4/edgcpp/compiler' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/9ca5f232d1d58ec7d7c599dfedf743f26c504fd5dac349d433fc6e7b6fa1a9d4/edgcpp/compiler' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(448) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/ahmd-sh/hntui' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Hntui – A TUI for Hacker News</a></p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49876760' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 25</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49876760' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 12</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Hntui is a Show HN project that implements a terminal user interface for Hacker News, enabling fast keyboard-driven reading in a lightweight app. It demonstrates how a well-crafted TUI can deliver a distraction-free browsing experience. A proof-of-concept worth watching for CLI UI enthusiasts.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Hntui 是一个 Show HN 项目，为 Hacker News 构建了一个终端文本用户界面，实现快捷的键盘驱动阅读。它展示了精心设计的文本界面如何提供更专注的浏览体验。对于命令行界面爱好者来说是一个值得关注的原型。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(450) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://locaterodeo.net/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>2026 International Utility Locate Rodeo</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>2026 国际公用事业定位竞技赛</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49927844' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 25</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49927844' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 5</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The International Utility Locate Rodeo returns, showcasing tools and techniques for locating underground utilities. Participants compete in accuracy, safety, and efficiency while vendors highlight new detection sensors, tracing methods, and standards. The event signals ongoing demand for reliable subsurface mapping in construction and utility work.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>2026 年国际公用事业定位竞技赛再次登场，展示地下管线定位的工具与方法。参赛者在精准度、安全性和效率方面竞争，厂商则展示新型探测传感器、追踪技术与行业标准。该赛事反映建筑和公用事业领域对可靠地下地图的持续需求。</p>

The piece outlines practical steps for developers and users to reclaim agency over devices and data.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>主张 Personal Computing 2.0，呼吁在人与计算机交互方式上掀起革命——以隐私为先、本地优先的软件，以及对用户的掌控。文中提出开发者和用户可以采取的具体步骤，重获对设备和数据的主导权。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49916997' data-tags='Show HN,Programming,Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(452) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/TantrixAuto/yantra' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Show HN: Yantra – an LALR(1) parser generator for C++</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Show HN：Yantra — 面向 C++ 的 LALR(1) 解析器生成器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49916997' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 24</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49916997' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 15</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Show HN'>Show HN</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Yantra is a new LALR(1) parser generator for C++. It aims to streamline building language parsers by emitting clean, idiomatic C++ code and parsing tables. For projects where tight integration with modern C++ tooling matters, it offers an alternative to traditional parser generators with a focus on ergonomics and performance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Yantra 是一个面向 C++ 的 LALR(1) 解析器生成器，旨在通过输出整洁、符合现代 C++ 风格的代码与解析表来简化解析器开发。在需要与现代 C++ 工具链紧密集成的场景中，它提供了一个对传统生成器的高效、易用的替代方案。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/bea8af58ef4d194525730a7526f43620bf6dfe9084d9615c1288d39e23ef1907/TantrixAuto/yantra' data-full='https://opengraph.githubassets.com/bea8af58ef4d194525730a7526f43620bf6dfe9084d9615c1288d39e23ef1907/TantrixAuto/yantra' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Co-leading a safety-focused bootcamp for legal and governance practitioners highlighted that AI risk management must translate technical concepts into regulatory terms. The experience underscores the need for governance-oriented risk assessments, practical playbooks, and cross-disciplinary collaboration to bridge safety research and real-world compliance.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>在面向法律与治理从业者的 AI 安全训练营中，跨学科协作暴露出将技术风险语言转译为监管术语的挑战。强调以治理为导向的风险评估、可操作的工具包，以及跨领域协作以把安全研究落地于合规实践。</p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A look at how long it typically takes to declare a bird extinct after the last sighting, with a median wait of about 36 years. The delay highlights challenges in conservation data, monitoring, and timely policy action.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>研究显示在最后一次观测后，宣布鸟类灭绝的中位等待时间约为36年。该延迟暴露出保护数据、监测与及时政策行动方面的挑战。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(473) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://quuxplusone.github.io/blog/2026/09/30/recursive-make-j/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Recursive `make` and `-j`</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>递归式 make 与 -j 的探讨</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49912094' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 17</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49912094' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 5</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A deep dive into why parallel builds can fail when you nest make invocations, and how to structure dependencies to avoid races. Practical tips for building large projects efficiently.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章解读了递归 make 与 -j 的工作原理及风险，解释在嵌套调用中如何避免并发冲突，并给出在大型项目中提高构建效率的实用建议。</p>

<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>More like SC-Forth-thousand, am I right? covers porting a Forth environment to the SG-1000 using RC2026, delivering a tiny Forth-powered development environment on retro hardware. The write-up discusses memory and IO constraints and celebrates retro-computing experimentation.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>题为 More like SC-Forth-thousand 的文章讲述在 RC2026 上将 Forth 环境移植到 SG-1000 的过程，实现了复古硬件上的紧凑 Forth 开发环境。文中讨论了内存、输入/输出等约束，以及复古计算机实验的魅力。</p>

<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>编程 ENIAC（2011）</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49918480' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 13</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49918480' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 0</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--sky' data-tag='Education'>Education</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A retrospective on programming ENIAC, detailing how technicians wired cables and set switches to implement instructions before stored-program computers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>对 ENIAC 编程的回顾介绍了通过布线和开关实现指令的时代，在存储程序计算机普及之前的做法。此文强调早期计算的动手性，以及这段历史如何为现代软件工程和编译原理奠定基础。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(484) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://tpy-lang.org/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>TurboPython – A Python-to-C++ Compiler</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>TurboPython——一个 Python 转 C++ 编译器</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49954875' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 12</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49954875' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 2</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='Programming'>Programming</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>TurboPython is a Python-to-C++ compiler aimed at accelerating certain workloads by translating Python code into optimized C++. It highlights a path toward performance gains without sacrificing Python familiarity, while outlining trade-offs in compatibility and tooling.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>TurboPython，是一个将 Python 转换为 C++ 的编译器，旨在通过翻译代码为优化后的 C++ 来提升特定工作负载的性能。它展示了在保持 Python 易用性的同时实现性能提升的可能性，同时也点出兼容性与工具链的权衡。</p>

<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49933069' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 12</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49933069' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 3</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>L-systems serve as a powerful formal grammar to generate complex, self-similar patterns—from decorative weevils to pizza topping layouts and even imagined matrilineal lineages—demonstrating how simple rules yield surprising complexity.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>L-系统作为一种强大的形式文法，能够从简单规则演化出复杂自相似的图案——从象鼻虫的生长模式到披萨配料的排列，甚至母系谱系的抽象结构，展现了简单规则如何产生意想不到的复杂性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://pic.blog.plover.com/misc/fibonacci-word-2/76676.png' data-full='https://pic.blog.plover.com/misc/fibonacci-word-2/76676.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A short post highlighting that port 8125, commonly used by StatsD, often has no listener in many environments, underscoring the importance of auditing standard ports and securing networks.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>简短文章指出 8125 端口（常用于 StatsD）在许多环境中常常没有侦听服务，强调对常用端口进行审计和加强网络安全的重要性。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49950288' data-tags='AI,Entertainment'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(488) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://twitter.com/robertvaradan/status/2106061989122334778' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI can clone your indie game, but not its soul</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 可以克隆你的独立游戏，但没有灵魂</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49950288' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 11</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49950288' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 5</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--purple' data-tag='Entertainment'>Entertainment</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Explains that AI can imitate art style, assets, and level design, enabling cloning of indie games, but struggles to replicate the original game&#x27;s soul—its unique pacing, narrative voice, and player experience. The takeaway: AI is a powerful tool for rapid iteration, but human authorship remains essential for authenticity.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>指出 AI 能模仿独立游戏的美术风格、资源和关卡设计，从而实现对原作的克隆，但难以复制其独特的灵魂——游戏的节奏、叙事风格与玩家体验。结论是，AI 是高效迭代的强大工具，但保持真实感仍需人类创作者。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://pbs.twimg.com/media/HTo6GQqXkAE6wKP?format=webp&amp;name=large' data-full='https://pbs.twimg.com/media/HTo6GQqXkAE6wKP?format=webp&amp;name=large' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-body'>

<p class='hn-title'>(491) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.swissinfo.ch/eng/glaciers-permafrost/a-fifth-of-swiss-alpine-ice-has-vanished-in-five-years/92132132' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>A fifth of Swiss Alpine ice has vanished in five years</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>五年内瑞士阿尔卑斯冰雪消失五分之一</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49928218' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 11</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49928218' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 0</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--emerald' data-tag='Energy'>Energy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--indigo' data-tag='DevOps'>DevOps</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>A fifth of Swiss Alpine ice has vanished in five years amid accelerating glacier retreat. The rapid loss threatens water resources, hydropower, and alpine ecosystems, while raising concerns about climate resilience for communities and infrastructure. Scientists emphasize the urgency of climate action and monitoring.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>五年内，瑞士阿尔卑斯山脉的冰川减少了五分之一，显示出快速的退缩趋势。冰雪消失威胁水资源、水电以及高山生态系统，并引发对社区与基础设施适应能力的担忧。科学家强调气候行动的紧迫性与持续监测的重要性。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.swissinfo.ch/content/wp-content/uploads/sites/13/2026/09/huss.jpg?ver=44f01ea6&amp;fit=1200,800' data-full='https://www.swissinfo.ch/content/wp-content/uploads/sites/13/2026/09/huss.jpg?ver=44f01ea6&amp;fit=1200,800' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<p class='hn-title'>(492) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.raspberrypi.com/news/price-increases-for-2gb-raspberry-pi-4-and-raspberry-pi-5/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Price Increases for 2GB Raspberry Pi 4 and Raspberry Pi 5</a></p>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Raspberry Pi Foundation announces price increases for 2GB Raspberry Pi 4 and the Raspberry Pi 5, citing cost pressures. The change could affect hobbyists, education programs, and the broader maker ecosystem, though the impact varies by region.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Raspberry Pi 基金会宣布 2GB Raspberry Pi 4 与 Raspberry Pi 5 的价格上涨，原因在于成本与供应链压力。此举将影响爱好者、教育项目及相关生态系统，地区差异也可能因定价调整而加大。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(493) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.reuters.com/legal/transactional/ai-borrowers-face-tough-sell-risky-corners-us-credit-market-2026-09-30/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>AI borrowers face tough sell in risky corners of US credit market</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>AI 借款人在美国高风险信贷市场难以获得融资</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49918147' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 11</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49918147' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 2</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--amber' data-tag='Finance'>Finance</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Lenders are cautious about AI-powered borrowers in riskier corners of the US credit market, tightening underwriting and reducing appetite for such borrowers.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>放贷机构对在高风险信贷领域使用 AI 的借款人持观望态度，信贷审核趋于严格，愿意提供的额度也在下降。文章强调了模型风险、审核透明度和市场环境如何共同影响 AI 驱动借款人在信贷市场中的获取融资能力。</p>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Fog-Bank archives the oldest webcam feeds, preserving a slice of early internet history and offering a glimpse into how public cameras shaped online culture and surveillance narratives.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>Fog-Bank 存档了最早期的网络摄像头画面，保存了网络历史的一角，并让人洞见早期公共摄像头如何影响网络文化与监控叙事。</p>

<p class='hn-title'>(495) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.theapplepost.com/2026/10/01/72882/apples-smart-home-camera-reportedly-wont-record-video/' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Apple&#x27;s smart home camera reportedly won&#x27;t record video</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>苹果的智能家居摄像头据称不会录制视频</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49928054' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 10</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49928054' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 14</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Privacy'>Privacy</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--slate' data-tag='Hardware'>Hardware</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--violet' data-tag='Data'>Data</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Reports claim Apple’s upcoming smart home camera will not record video, instead relying on on-device processing and privacy-preserving analytics. The design choice could reshape home security expectations and cloud dependencies. If true, it may set a precedent for privacy-first IoT hardware.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>据传苹果的智能家居摄像头将不进行视频录制，而改为本地处理和隐私保护分析。这一设计可能改变家庭安防体验与对云端存储的依赖程度。若属实，或为注重隐私的物联网硬件设立新基准。</p>

</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49957028' data-tags='Open Source'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(496) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://github.com/rcalixte/ncdu' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Ncdu: An Updated Fork</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>Ncdu：更新后的分叉版本</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49957028' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 8</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49957028' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 2</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--green' data-tag='Open Source'>Open Source</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Introduces a refreshed fork of ncdu with new features, bug fixes, and potential performance improvements. The update keeps the familiar interface while expanding disk usage analysis capabilities.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>介绍了 ncdu 的更新分叉，带来新功能和错误修复，可能提升性能。保持原有界面的同时扩展了磁盘使用分析的能力。</p>

<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49933755' data-tags='AI,Science'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(497) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.oneusefulthing.org/p/the-dot-and-the-swarm' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>The dot and the Swarm: Benefitting from the bitter lesson</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>点与群：从苦涩教训中获益</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49933755' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 8</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49933755' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 4</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--blue' data-tag='AI'>AI</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--teal' data-tag='Science'>Science</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>The piece argues that distributed, swarm-like approaches can harness the &#x27;bitter lesson&#x27; of AI progress—favoring scalable, simple mechanisms over overly engineered centralized systems when solving complex problems.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>文章主张在 AI 进展的苦涩教训中，分布式/群体式方法更易实现可扩展性，强调以简洁的机制优于过度设计的中心化系统来解决复杂问题。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!Narw!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Fe3014425-47c2-47aa-848b-3528f4dc9517_2912x1632.png' data-full='https://substackcdn.com/image/fetch/$s_!Narw!,w_1200,h_675,c_fill,f_jpg,q_auto:good,fl_progressive:steep,g_auto/https%3A%2F%2Fsubstack-post-media.s3.amazonaws.com%2Fpublic%2Fimages%2Fe3014425-47c2-47aa-848b-3528f4dc9517_2912x1632.png' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
<div class='hn-card hn-card-compact hn-no-collapse' id='story-49952207' data-tags='Security,Legal'>
<div class='hn-body'>
<button class='hn-digest-toggle' type='button' aria-expanded='false' title='Show summary and image' aria-label='Show summary and image'><span aria-hidden='true'>⌄</span></button>
<p class='hn-title'>(498) <a class='hn-top-title-link' href='https://www.europol.europa.eu/media-press/newsroom/news/teenager-suspected-of-leading-killsec-ransomware-group-law-enforcement-seizes-servers-and-leak-site' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>Teenager suspected of leading KillSec ransomware group</a></p>
<p class='hn-meta hn-text-zh'>嫌疑少年被指控领导 KillSec 勒索软件组织</p>
<div class='hn-tags'><a class='hn-stat hn-stat-score' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49952207' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#9650; 7</a> <a class='hn-stat hn-stat-comments' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=49952207' target='_blank' rel='noopener noreferrer'>&#128172; 0</a> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--red' data-tag='Security'>Security</span> <span class='hn-tag hn-tag--orange' data-tag='Legal'>Legal</span></div>
<div class='hn-digest-details' hidden>
<p class='hn-text-en hn-digest-summary'>Authorities allege a teenager led KillSec ransomware, with servers seized and the group&#x27;s dark web presence dismantled. The case underscores the growing reach of youth-operated ransomware networks and the importance of international law enforcement.</p>
<p class='hn-text-zh hn-digest-summary'>警方指控一名少年领导 KillSec 勒索软件组织，相关服务器被扣押、黑暗网络入口被关闭。案件凸显青少年参与的勒索网络规模及执法力量的持续打击。</p>
<img class='hn-img hn-digest-img' src='https://www.europol.europa.eu/cms/sites/default/files/styles/900x/public/images/261001-Op-Killswitch-Lead.jpg?itok=sQn806mb' data-full='https://www.europol.europa.eu/cms/sites/default/files/styles/900x/public/images/261001-Op-Killswitch-Lead.jpg?itok=sQn806mb' alt='preview image' loading='lazy' decoding='async'/>
</div>
</div>
</div>
